Our results show that shallow machine learning models with appropriate feature engineering achieve strong overall performance, reaching up to 71% overall score averaged over accuracy, F1-score, and ROC AUC metrics, while transformer-based models achieve slightly lower overall score (68%) averaged over the same metrics, but demonstrate better generalization on minority reflection classes. These findings highlight the continued relevance of classical methods for low-resource settings and the robustness of transformer models for imbalanced classification. The proposed dataset and experimental insights provide a solid foundation for future research on automated reflective analysis in Hungarian and other morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [75] [HalluScan: A Systematic Benchmark for Detecting and Mitigating Hallucinations in Instruction-Following LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02443)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluScan是一个全面的幻觉检测与缓解基准框架，包含72种配置、6种检测方法、4个开源模型家族和3个领域。提出了HalluScore复合指标（与人类判断的Pearson相关系数r=0.41）、自适应检测路由ADR（成本降低2.0倍，AUROC仅下降0.1%）和系统化错误级联分解。实验表明NLI Verification取得最高AUROC 0.88，RAV为0.66。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在各类NLP任务中表现出色，但仍存在生成幻觉内容的问题（事实错误、偏离上下文、不符合用户指令），现有评估方法缺乏系统性和全面性。

Method: 构建HalluScan基准框架，涵盖6种幻觉检测方法、4个开源模型家族、3个领域共72种配置；提出HalluScore复合评估指标；设计自适应检测路由ADR算法；进行错误级联分解分析。

Result: HalluScore与人类专家判断的Pearson相关系数为0.41；ADR实现2.0倍成本降低，AUROC仅下降0.1%；NLI Verification取得最高AUROC 0.88，RAV为0.66；不同领域幻觉错误类型存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM幻觉检测提供了系统化基准框架，HalluScore可有效评估幻觉程度，ADR能在几乎不影响性能的前提下大幅降低成本，且不同领域幻觉模式差异需要领域特定的检测策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse natural language processing tasks, yet they remain susceptible to hallucinations -- generating content that is factually incorrect, unfaithful to provided context, or misaligned with user instructions. We present HalluScan, a comprehensive benchmark framework that systematically evaluates hallucination detection and mitigation across 72 configurations spanning 6 detection methods, 4 open-weight model families, and 3 diverse domains. We introduce three key contributions: (1) HalluScore, a novel composite metric that achieves a Pearson correlation of r = 0.41 with human expert judgments; (2) Adaptive Detection Routing (ADR), an intelligent routing algorithm achieving 2.0x cost reduction with only 0.1% AUROC degradation; and (3) systematic error cascade decomposition revealing substantial variation in hallucination error types across domains. Our experiments reveal that NLI Verification achieves the highest overall AUROC of 0.88, while RAV achieves the second-highest AUROC of 0.66.

</details>


### [76] [PC-MNet: Dual-Level Congruity Modeling for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection via Polarity-Modulated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02447)
*Maoheng Li,Ling Zhou,Xiaohua Huang,Rubing Huang,Wenming Zheng,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种标量一致路由机制与先验引导上下文图相结合的多模态讽刺检测方法，通过不一致感知对比学习驱动的两阶段非对称优化来解耦功能纠缠，在MUStARD基准上取得SOTA，Macro-F1提升3.14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖基于相似性的注意力机制和统一的晚期融合策略，功能纠缠限制了传统晚期融合对文本与非语言线索间语用不一致性的建模能力。

Method: 提出标量一致路由机制（Scalar Congruity Routing）和先验引导上下文图（Prior-guided Contextual Graph），通过不一致感知对比学习（Inconsistency-aware Contrastive Learning）驱动两阶段非对称优化，选择性地融合最具判别力的多粒度证据。

Result: 在MUStARD基准及其消除虚假相关的平衡数据集上，Macro-F1超过最强多模态基线3.14%，达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过架构上隔离原子冲突、组合冲突和上下文冲突，该工作为建模人类交流中微妙的语用不一致性提供了一种鲁棒且解耦的范式。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection, which aims to precisely identify pragmatic incongruities between literal text and nonverbal cues, has gained substantial attention in multimodal understanding. Recent advancements have predominantly relied on naïve similarity-based attention mechanisms and uniform late fusion strategies.Furthermore, given that functional entanglement restricts traditional late fusions, we incorporate a scalar congruity routing mechanism and a prior-guided contextual graph. This mechanism anchors a generalized incongruity manifold through a two-stage asymmetric optimization driven by inconsistency-aware contrastive learning, selectively fusing only the most discriminative multi-granularity evidence. Extensive experiments on the \texttt{MUStARD} benchmark and its spurious-correlation-mitigated balanced datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves new state-of-the-art performance, surpassing the strongest multimodal baseline by a substantial 3.14\% improvement in Macro-F1. By architecturally isolating atomic, composition, and contextual conflicts. This work provides a robust, decoupled paradigm for modeling subtle pragmatic incongruities in human communication.

</details>


### [77] [Leveraging Argument Structure to Predict Content Hatefulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02457)
*Nicolas Benjamin Ocampo,Davide Ceolin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用论证结构（前提和结论）分析白人民族主义论坛消息，通过检查论证组件的可核查性和仇恨性注释来识别整条消息的仇恨程度，F1最高达96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息混乱（包括仇恨言论、虚假信息、错误信息等）是一个影响广泛的社会问题，需要综合性解决方案。本文旨在探索论证结构能否提供连接这些不同问题维度的相关信息。

Method: 使用WSF-ARG+数据集（白人民族主义论坛消息，已标注论证结构），利用论证组件（前提和结论）的可核查性和仇恨性注释来推断整条消息的仇恨程度。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法有良好效果，F1值最高达96%。

Conclusion: 论证结构分析在识别仇恨内容和应对信息混乱方面有潜力，未来可进一步扩展该研究方向。

Abstract: Information disorder is a challenging phenomenon that affects society at large. This phenomenon entails the diffusion of misleading, misinforming, and hateful content online. In different contexts, one aspect of the problem may prevail, but overall, this is a broad problem that requires comprehensive solutions. While each dimension of the problem (hate speech, disinformation, misinformation, etc.) requires in-depth analysis, in this paper, we look into the possibility of argument structure to provide relevant information to link these different areas of the problem. In particular, we focus on the WSF-ARG+ dataset, which consists of white supremacy forum messages annotated in terms of argument structure (premises and conclusion). There, we leverage the checkworthiness and hatefulness annotations of the argument components to obtain insights into the hatefulness of the whole message. Our results show promising insights (up to 96% F1), indicating the possibility of extending this direction in the future to tackle hateful content identification and information disorder countering.

</details>


### [78] [ATLAS: Article Tracking, Linking, and Analysis of Swedish Encyclopedias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02466)
*Albin Andersson,Salam Jonasson,Fredrik Wastring,Pierre Nugues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套流水线，用于恢复数字化百科全书的文本结构，包括提取词条、分类实体、跨版本匹配以及链接到Wikidata，并在瑞典百科全书《Nordisk familjebok》的四个主要版本上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧百科全书的数字化通常仅停留在OCR层面，未利用其底层结构，导致难以追踪不同版本间的知识演变。因此需要一种自动化方法来恢复文本结构，便于知识保存和传播研究。

Method: 构建了一条包含四个步骤的流水线：提取词条并识别条目；对实体进行分类；跨版本匹配条目；将条目链接到Wikidata。应用于瑞典百科全书《Nordisk familjebok》的四个主要版本（1876–1951）。

Result: 词条提取F1分数97.8%，词条分类F1分数93.4%；跨版本匹配精确率93%；Wikidata链接精确率85%，召回率16.5%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，自动化方法处理数字化历史知识是可行的，有助于促进通用知识的保存和理解知识传播。

Abstract: The digitization of old encyclopedias represents an important step to improve access to historically structured knowledge. Often, however, this process does not go beyond an optical character recognition, leaving all the underlying structure unexploited. In addition, many encyclopedias had multiple editions reflecting the evolution of knowledge. The lack of structure in the raw text makes it difficult to track changes across these editions. In this work, we built a pipeline to restore the text structure, where we extract the headwords and identify entries; categorize the entities; match entries across editions; and link entries to a Wikidata item. We applied this pipeline to the four major editions of \textit{Nordisk familjebok}, an authoritative Swedish encyclopedia published between 1876 and 1951. We could extract the headwords with an F1 score of 97.8\% and we obtained an F1 score of 93.4\% on the headword classification. On a small-scale evaluation, we reached a 93\% precision on the cross-edition matching, 85\% precision and 16.5\% recall on the Wikidata linking. This shows that an automated approach to digitized historical knowledge is possible. This should facilitate the preservation of general knowledge and the understanding of knowledge transmission. The datasets and programs are available online.

</details>


### [79] [Accurate Legal Reasoning at Scale: Neuro-Symbolic Offloading and Structural Auditability for Robust Legal Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02472)
*Stanisław Sójka,Witold Kowalczyk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"摊销智能"（Amortized Intelligence）的神经符号方法，利用LLM一次性将法律文本翻译为确定性自主合约语言（DACL）的图中间表示，随后通过确定性图执行进行裁决，相比前沿大型推理模型（LRMs），在保持近乎完美一致性的同时，计算成本降低超90%，且满足法律裁决的可审计性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本中的计算性法律条款需要复杂逻辑理解，而前沿大型推理模型存在推理错误率高、推理成本高昂的问题，难以构建生产级系统。

Method: 提出摊销智能方法，使用LLM仅一次将法律文本翻译为DACL（带类型的图中间表示），裁决阶段依赖确定性图执行，并生成可视化可审计的执行轨迹。

Result: 相比GPT-5.2和Gemini 3 Pro等运行时LRM基线，DACL智能体实现了近乎完美的一致性，缓解了概率模型中的"推理悬崖"问题，在高吞吐工作流中计算成本降低超90%。

Conclusion: DACL-based智能体通过神经符号方法有效平衡了法律裁决中的准确性、成本效益与可审计性要求，为法律文本计算条款的自动化处理提供了可行的生产级方案。

Abstract: Legal texts often contain computational legal clauses--provisions whose understanding requires complex logic. While frontier Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) can describe such clauses, building production-ready systems is limited by reasoning errors and the high cost of inference. We propose Amortized Intelligence, a neuro-symbolic approach where we use an LLM once to translate a legal text into Deterministic Autonomous Contract Language (DACL): a typed graph intermediate representation. Adjudication then relies on deterministic graph executions with a visually auditable trace. In comparison against runtime LRM baselines (including GPT-5.2 and Gemini 3 Pro), our DACL-based Agent achieves near-perfect consistency and mitigates the "reasoning cliff" observed in probabilistic models. The system reduces compute costs by over 90% in high-volume workflows while satisfying the strict auditability requirements of legal adjudication.

</details>


### [80] [A multilingual hallucination benchmark: MultiWikiQHalluA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02504)
*Freja Thoresen,Dan Saattrup Smart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多语言（306种）中的忠实性幻觉问题，利用MultiWikiQA和LettuceDetect框架生成合成幻觉数据集，训练了30种欧洲语言的token级幻觉分类器，并在英语、丹麦语、德语和冰岛语上评估了五种模型的幻觉率。结果显示，小模型（如Qwen3-0.6B）幻觉率高达60%，低资源语言（尤其是冰岛语）幻觉率普遍更高，大模型（如cogito系列）表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉评估大多聚焦英语，尚不清楚相关发现能否迁移到低资源语言，因此有必要进行多语言幻觉评估研究。

Method: 利用多语言MultiWikiQA数据集，结合LettuceDetect框架为306种语言生成合成幻觉数据，训练30种欧洲语言的token级幻觉分类器，并在英语、丹麦语、德语和冰岛语上评估Qwen3-0.6B、Qwen3-14B、Gemma-3-12B-IT、cogito-v1-preview-qwen-32B和cogito-v1-preview-llama-70B五种模型的幻觉率。

Result: Qwen3-0.6B幻觉率最高（高达60%的答案至少包含一次幻觉，冰岛语峰值）；cogito系列模型（32B和70B）在多数语言上表现最佳；低资源语言（特别是冰岛语）的幻觉率持续高于高资源语言。

Conclusion: 多语言幻觉评估表明，模型规模和语言资源丰富度均显著影响幻觉率，低资源语言面临更严重的幻觉问题，需进一步研究缓解策略。

Abstract: Most hallucination evaluations focus on English, leaving it unclear whether findings transfer to lower-resource languages. We investigate faithfulness hallucinations, defined as model-generated content that is fluent and plausible but diverges from the provided input or is internally inconsistent. Leveraging the multilingual MultiWikiQA dataset, we utilize the LettuceDetect framework to create synthetic hallucination datasets for 306 languages, from which we train token-level hallucination classifiers for 30 European languages. In this work, we present evaluations of model hallucinations on a selection of languages: English, Danish, German, and Icelandic. Using these classifiers, we evaluate the hallucination rates for Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-14B, Gemma-3-12B-IT, cogito-v1-preview-qwen-32B, and cogito-v1-preview-llama-70B. Our classifiers reveal notably higher hallucination rates for Qwen3-0.6B (up to 60\% of answers containing at least one hallucination, peaking in Icelandic) and generally lower rates for larger models, with cogito-v1-preview-qwen-32B and cogito-v1-preview-llama-70B performing best on most languages. Hallucination rates are consistently higher for lower-resource languages, particularly Icelandic.

</details>


### [81] [Revisiting Semantic Role Labeling: Efficient Structured Inference with Dependency-Informed Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02505)
*Sangpil Youm,Leah Jones,Bonnie J. Dorr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个现代化的编码器框架用于结构化语义角色标注（SRL），该框架在保持显式谓词-论元结构的同时，实现了10倍的推理加速。基于BERT-base的模型达到了可比的预测性能，RoBERTa和DeBERTa进一步提升了F1分数。研究通过依存关系诊断方法分析了跨度不一致性，并考察了LLM在结构信号下的表示行为，最后展示了该框架在多语言SRL投影中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SRL系统依赖已进入维护模式的AllenNLP，难以兼容新型编码器架构与现代推理需求；同时LLM虽在NLP中占据主导，但缺乏显式的结构约束和系统性解释机制。

Method: 提出一种现代化编码器框架，采用BERT/RoBERTa/DeBERTa作为编码器，保留显式谓词-论元结构；采用依存信息诊断方法分析跨度不一致性，并进行LLM表示层面的分析。

Result: BERT-base取得可比预测性能，RoBERTa和DeBERTa进一步提升F1分数；推理速度提升10倍；依存线索主要提升结构稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持显式结构的同时显著提升了推理效率，支持多语言SRL投影等下游应用，为结构化语义分析提供了现代化解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic Role Labeling (SRL) provides an explicit representation of predicate-argument structure, capturing linguistically grounded relations such as who did what to whom. While recent NLP progress has been dominated by large language models (LLMs), these systems often rely on implicit semantic representations, often lacking explicit structural constraints and systematic explanatory mechanisms. Traditionally, SRL systems have often relied on AllenNLP; however, the framework entered maintenance mode in December 2022, limiting compatibility with evolving encoder architectures and modern inference requirements. We revisit structured SRL modeling, introducing a modernized encoder-based framework that preserves explicit predicate-argument structure while enabling inference 10 times faster. Using BERT-base, the model attains comparable predictive performance, and RoBERTa and DeBERTa further improve F1 performance within the same framework. We adopt a dependency-informed diagnostic methodology to characterize span-level inconsistencies and conduct a representation-level analysis of LLM behavior under dependency-informed structural signals. Results indicate that dependency cues primarily improve structural stability. Finally, we illustrate how the framework's explicit predicate-argument structure can support multilingual SRL projection as a downstream application.

</details>


### [82] [Benchmarking Retrieval Strategies for Biomedical Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02520)
*Devi Prasad Bal,Subhashree Puhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种检索策略（密集向量搜索、混合BM25+密集检索、交叉编码器重排序、多查询扩展和最大边际相关性）在生物医学RAG问答管道中的表现。交叉编码器重排序获得最佳综合评分(0.827)和最高上下文精确度(0.852)，多查询扩展的上下文精确度最弱(0.671)。所有RAG策略在答案相关性上均远超无上下文基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险生物医学领域中，不同检索策略对RAG性能的影响缺乏受控的、多指标的系统比较研究。现有工作未能在固定生成模型、向量库和嵌入模型的条件下，单独评估检索策略的贡献。

Method: 在固定生成模型（GPT-4o-mini）、向量库（ChromaDB）和嵌入模型（text-embedding-3-small）的RAG管道中，比较五种检索策略：密集向量搜索、混合BM25+密集检索、交叉编码器重排序、多查询扩展和MMR。使用BioASQ基准的250个问答对，采用DeepEval的四个指标（上下文精确度、上下文召回率、忠实度、答案相关性）评估，并包含无上下文消融实验作为下界。

Result: 交叉编码器重排序综合得分最高(0.827)，上下文精确度最高(0.852)；多查询扩展上下文精确度最低(0.671)；密集基线综合得分0.822，与最佳策略仅差0.005；所有RAG条件答案相关性(0.658-0.701)显著优于无上下文基线(0.287)。

Conclusion: 交叉编码器重排序通过查询-文档交互实现了可测量的检索增益；多查询扩展的朴素查询多样化引入检索噪声；MMR在多样性与答案相关性之间存在权衡；密集检索基线已接近最佳性能。全部代码和超参数已公开。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offers a well-established path to grounding large language model (LLM) outputs in external knowledge, yet the question of which retrieval strategy works best in a high-stakes domain such as biomedicine has not received the controlled, multi-metric treatment it deserves. This paper presents a systematic empirical comparison of five retrieval strategies -- Dense Vector Search, Hybrid BM25 + Dense retrieval, Cross-Encoder Reranking, Multi-Query Expansion, and Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) -- within a biomedical question-answering RAG pipeline. All strategies share a fixed generation model (GPT-4o-mini), a common vector store (ChromaDB), and OpenAI's text-embedding-3-small embeddings, ensuring that observed differences are attributable to retrieval alone. Evaluation is conducted on 250 question-answer pairs drawn from a preprocessed subset of the BioASQ benchmark (rag-mini-bioasq) using four DeepEval metrics: contextual precision, contextual recall, faithfulness, and answer relevancy, each reported with 95% confidence intervals. A no-context ablation is included as a lower bound. Cross-Encoder Reranking achieves the best composite score (0.827) and highest contextual precision (0.852), confirming that query-document interaction yields measurable retrieval gains. Multi-Query Expansion, despite its recall-oriented design, produces the weakest contextual precision (0.671), suggesting naive query diversification introduces retrieval noise. MMR sacrifices answer relevancy for diversity, while the Dense baseline (composite 0.822) falls within 0.005 points of the top strategy. All RAG conditions dramatically outperform the no-context ablation on answer relevancy (0.658-0.701 vs. 0.287), confirming the practical value of retrieval. The full pipeline, hyperparameters, and evaluation code are publicly available.

</details>


### [83] [SemEval-2026 Task 7: Everyday Knowledge Across Diverse Languages and Cultures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02601)
*Nedjma Ousidhoum,Junho Myung,Carla Perez-Almendros,Jiho Jin,Amr Keleg,Meriem Beloucif,Yi Zhou,Rodrigo Agerri,Vladimir Araujo,Naomi Baes,James Barry,Joanne Boisson,Nancy F. Chen,Christine de Kock,Aleksandra Edwards,Joseba Fernandez de Landa,Mohamed Fazli Imam,Huda Hakami,Shu-Kai Hsieh,Joseph Marvin Imperial,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Zhengyuan Liu,Chenyang Lyu,Younes Samih,Johan Sjons,Bryan Tan,Asahi Ushio,Weihua Zheng,Alice Oh,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一项关于评估LLM和NLP系统在多语言和多文化环境下适应性的共享任务，基于扩展的BLEnD基准，涵盖30多种语言-文化对（主要为低资源语言），设有简答题和选择题两个赛道，吸引了140多名注册参与者，最终收到62个团队提交和19篇系统描述论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM和NLP系统在多语言、多文化环境下的适应性评估不足，尤其缺乏针对低资源语言和未被充分代表文化的系统性评估基准和共享任务。

Method: 扩展了原有的BLEnD基准，覆盖30多种语言-文化对（以低资源语言为主）；设置两个评估赛道：简答题(SAQ)和选择题(MCQ)；参与者仅允许使用基准数据进行评估，禁止用于训练、微调或小样本学习；允许提交任意NLP系统和建模策略。

Result: 超过140名注册参与者，最终62个团队提交结果，19篇系统描述论文；对最佳系统和常见方法进行了分析和报告。

Conclusion: 讨论了低资源语言和未被充分代表文化中模型行为评估、错位问题和方法论视角方面的开放挑战与共享见解。

Abstract: We present our shared task on evaluating the adaptability of LLMs and NLP systems across multiple languages and cultures. The task data consist of an extended version of our manually constructed BLEnD benchmark (Myung et al. 2024), covering more than 30 language-culture pairs, predominantly representing low-resource languages spoken across multiple continents. As the task is designed strictly for evaluation, participants were not permitted to use the data for training, fine-tuning, few-shot learning, or any other form of model modification. Our task includes two tracks: (a) Short-Answer Questions (SAQ) and (b) Multiple-Choice Questions (MCQ). Participants were required to predict labels and were allowed to submit any NLP system and adopt diverse modelling strategies, provided that the benchmark was used solely for evaluation. The task attracted more than 140 registered participants, and we received final submissions from 62 teams, along with 19 system description papers. We report the results and present an analysis of the best-performing systems and the most commonly adopted approaches. Furthermore, we discuss shared insights into open questions and challenges related to evaluation, misalignment, and methodological perspectives on model behaviour in low-resource languages and for under-represented cultures.

</details>


### [84] [Dependency Parsing Across the Resource Spectrum: Evaluating Architectures on High and Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02608)
*Kevin Guan,Happy Buzaaba,Christiane Fellbaum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在低资源场景下，Biaffine LSTM 持续优于 Transformer 模型（如 AfroXLMR-large 和 RemBERT），Transformer 的优势随训练数据增加才恢复；形态复杂度是 Transformer 相对劣势的次要预测因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高资源语言中 Transformer 模型在依存句法分析上取得 SOTA，但其在低资源环境下的优势是否依然成立尚不明确，尤其针对非洲语言等低资源语种。

Method: 在10种类型多样的语言上比较四种句法分析器：Biaffine LSTM、Stack-Pointer Network、AfroXLMR-large 和 RemBERT，并控制语料库规模，测量形态复杂度（MATTR）。

Result: Biaffine LSTM 在低资源条件下始终优于 Transformer 模型；Transformer 的优势随训练数据增加才恢复，交叉点恰好落在低资源语言树库的典型数据量范围内；形态复杂度（MATTR）是除语料规模外 Transformer 相对劣势的显著次要预测因素。

Conclusion: 在低资源场景下，Biaffine LSTM 更适合作为句法工具开发的基础模型，直到积累足够的标注数据来发挥预训练 Transformer 的表示能力。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve state-of-the-art dependency parsing for high-resource languages, yet their advantage over simpler architectures in low-resource settings remains poorly understood. We evaluate four parsers -- the Biaffine LSTM, Stack-Pointer Network, AfroXLMR-large, and RemBERT -- across ten typologically diverse languages, with a focus on low-resource African languages. We find that the Biaffine LSTM consistently outperforms transformer models in low-resource regimes, with transformers recovering their advantage as training data increases. The crossover falls within a resource range typical of treebanks for under-resourced languages. Morphological complexity (measured via MATTR) emerges as a significant secondary predictor of transformers' relative disadvantage after controlling for corpus size. These results indicate that the Biaffine LSTM may be better suited for syntactic tool development in low-resource regimes until sufficient annotated data is available to leverage the representational capacity of pre-trained transformers.

</details>


### [85] [Beating the Style Detector: Three Hours of Agentic Research on the AI-Text Arms Race](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02620)
*Andreas Maier,Moritz Zaiss,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用现代智能体研究框架，在人类研究者仅作为审核者的情况下，完整复现并扩展了一项关于个人风格后编辑LLM草稿的ACL 2026研究。结果精准复现了原始发现，并进一步揭示了前沿LLM在风格迁移和对抗AI检测方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NLP实证研究复现耗时数周，本文旨在验证在自动化智能体研究框架下，能否高效、精准地复现并扩展复杂的实证研究，减少人力投入并提高可重复性。

Method: 使用智能体研究框架自动化执行实验流程；复现7个预注册假设；采用无泄露的留出协议评估GPT-5.5和Claude Opus 4.7的风格迁移能力；通过留作者线性SVM和LUAR-MUD嵌入进行AI文本检测；进行T=20轮对抗反馈迭代测试检测规避能力。

Result: 成功复现了人感知自我相似性与嵌入测量自我相似性之间的显著相关性（r=+0.244, p<10⁻⁸）。前沿LLM（GPT-5.5和Claude Opus 4.7）能闭合71%-75%的风格差距，远超人类后编辑的24%。AI检测方面，GPT-5.5的检测主要基于长度混淆，而Opus的检测则体现真正的风格特征。经过20轮对抗反馈，Opus智能体可使两个测试样本被分类为人类，并将所有样本的分类边界缩小一个数量级。

Conclusion: 智能体研究框架可大幅提升实证NLP研究的复现效率；前沿LLM在风格模仿上已超越人类后编辑水平；在已知检测器面前，前沿LLM能以中等成本有效降低自身的AI检测概率，这对AI文本检测的可靠性提出了挑战。

Abstract: Reproducing an empirical NLP study used to take weeks. Given the released data and a modern agentic-research harness, we redo every experiment of a recent ACL\,2026 study on personal-style post-editing of LLM drafts -- and add three new ones -- with the human investigator acting only as a reviewer-in-the-loop. We reproduce all seven preregistered hypotheses and recover the paper's headline correlation between perceived self-similarity and embedding-measured self-similarity to three decimal places ($r{=}{+}0.244$, $p{<}10^{-8}$, $n{=}648$). Under a leakage-free held-out protocol, GPT-5.5 and Claude\,Opus\,4.7 close $71$--$75\,\%$ of the style gap to the same-author ceiling on $324$ paired tasks, against $24\,\%$ for the human post-edit, and beat the human post-edit on $\sim$$80\,\%$ of tasks. We then frame the same data as an AI-text detection arms race. A leave-authors-out linear SVM on LUAR-MUD embeddings reaches AUC $0.93$--$1.00$ across approaches; six diagnostics show that GPT-5.5 detection is mostly a length confound while Opus detection is a genuine stylistic signature. Given $T{=}20$ feedback iterations against the frozen detector, an Opus agent flips two of five held-out test mimics to the human half-space and shrinks every margin by an order of magnitude. With moderate effort against a known detector, a frontier LLM can already efficiently lower its own AI-detection probability. All code, $648$ mimic drafts, trained detectors, diagnostics, and adversarial trajectories are released.

</details>


### [86] [Synthetic Users, Real Differences: an Evaluation Framework for User Simulation in Multi-Turn Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02624)
*Yu Lu Liu,Hyokun Yun,Tanya Roosta,Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RealSim评估框架，从8个维度（交际功能、用户状态、消息表面形式）对真实与模拟用户-聊天机器人对话进行分布级对比，发现模拟用户难以捕捉真实用户的沟通摩擦，可能导致评估过于乐观，且不同领域表现存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟真实性评估方法仅停留在单轮对话层面的粗粒度质量信号，缺乏系统化的细粒度分布级评估，需要更严格的评估框架来支持模拟对话的真实性验证。

Method: 提出RealSim评估框架，从8个维度（交际功能、用户状态、消息表面形式）对真实与模拟对话进行分布级对比；构建包含1000轮多轮任务导向型真实对话的数据集，覆盖16个领域。

Result: 模拟用户难以捕捉真实用户引入的沟通摩擦，基于此类模拟的评估可能过于乐观；不同领域的模拟表现存在差异，表明可能需要领域特定的用户模拟器。

Conclusion: RealSim框架为模拟对话真实性提供了更细粒度、分布级的评估方法；当前模拟器在捕捉真实用户行为方面存在不足，未来需开发领域特定的模拟器以提高评估可靠性。

Abstract: There is growing interest in exploring user simulation as an alternative to gathering and scoring real user-chatbot interactions for AI chatbot evaluation. For this purpose, it is important to ensure the realism of the simulation, i.e., the extent to which simulated dialogues reflect real dialogues users have with chatbots. Most existing methods evaluating simulation realism produce coarse quality signal and remain solely at the level of individual dialogues. To support more rigorous evaluation in this area, we propose realsim, an evaluation framework that enables practitioners to take a distributional view of real vs. simulated dialogues along 8 dimensions, covering attributes related to the communicative functions of the interaction, user states, and the surface form of user messages. We then instantiate the framework with a curated dataset of 1K multi-turn task-focused real user-chatbot dialogues that cover 16 domains of chatbot applications. Overall, we find that simulated users tend to struggle at capturing communication frictions that real users introduce to interactions, which could make evaluations based on such simulations overly optimistic. We also observe variability in performance across different domains, which may indicate a need for domain-specific user simulators.

</details>


### [87] [mdok-style at SemEval-2026 Task 9: Finetuning LLMs for Multilingual Polarization Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02695)
*Dominik Macko,Alok Debnath,Jakub Simko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对SemEval-2026 Task 9多语言极化检测任务，采用QLoRA参数高效微调技术对中等规模LLM进行序列分类微调，并通过匿名化、大小写转换及同形字符替换等数据增强策略提升多语言（22种语言）检测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线极化现象常伴随仇恨言论、攻击性话语和社会分裂，对其早期检测对于构建更安全、更具包容性的在线空间至关重要。

Method: 使用QLoRA参数高效微调技术对中等规模大语言模型进行序列分类任务的微调；训练数据包含22种语言，并采用匿名化、小写化、大写化及同形字符替换等数据增强策略。

Result: 通过数据增强策略提升了多语言极化检测的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: QLoRA微调结合多模态数据增强可有效应对多语言、多文化、多事件的极化检测任务。

Abstract: SemEval-2026 Task 9 is focused on multilingual polarization detection. Specifically, it covers the identification of multilingual, multicultural and multievent polarization along three axes (in subtasks), namely detection, type, and manifestation. Online polarization presents a concern, because it is often followed by hate speech, offensive discourse, and social fragmentation. Therefore, its detection before it escalates is crucial for a safer and more inclusive online space. We have coped with this SemEval task by finetuning mid-size LLMs for the sequence-classification task using the QLoRA parameter-efficient finetuning technique. The training data augmented the multilingual (22 languages) training sets by anonymized, lower-cased, upper-cased, and homoglyphied counterparts, making the detection more robust.

</details>


### [88] [mdok-style at SemEval-2026 Task 10: Finetuning LLMs for Conspiracy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02712)
*Dominik Macko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据增强和自训练的Qwen3-32B微调系统，用于SemEval-2026 Task 10的阴谋论检测任务，最终在52个提交系统中排名第8（85百分位），证明了原用于机器生成文本检测的方法可迁移至阴谋论检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SemEval-2026 Task 10聚焦于阴谋论检测，即判断Reddit评论是否表达阴谋论信念。训练数据规模较小是主要挑战，需要有效的方法来应对数据稀疏问题。

Method: 采用数据增强和自训练策略应对少量训练数据，在此基础上微调Qwen3-32B模型，将其应用于二元文本分类任务。该方法最初来源于机器生成文本检测领域。

Result: 在52个提交系统中排名第8，处于85百分位，表现极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 源于机器生成文本检测的方法可成功迁移至阴谋论检测任务，验证了跨任务方法的有效性。

Abstract: SemEval-2026 Task 10 is focused on conspiracy detection. Specifically, the goal is to detect whether a Reddit comment expresses a conspiracy belief. Our submitted mdok-style system utilizes data augmentation and self-training (to cope with a rather small amount of training data) to finetune the Qwen3-32B model for a binary text-classification task. The submitted system is very competitive, ranking in the 85th percentile (8th out of 52 submissions). The results shown that our approach, which originated in machine-generated text detection, can be used for conspiracy detection as well.

</details>


### [89] [FlexSQL: Flexible Exploration and Execution Make Better Text-to-SQL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02815)
*Quang Hieu Pham,Yang He,Ping Nie,Canwen Xu,Davood Rafiei,Yuepeng Wang,Xi Ye,Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlexSQL 是一种基于灵活数据库交互原则的 Text-to-SQL 智能体，通过动态探索 schema、检查数据值和运行验证查询，生成多样化执行计划并支持两级修复机制。在 Spider2-Snow 基准上，使用 gpt-oss-120b 达到 65.4% 分数，超越使用更强更大模型（如 gpt-o3、DeepSeek-R1）的强基线，并能为通用编码智能体带来超过 10% 的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 Text-to-SQL 系统采用固定流水线：仅一次性检索 schema 元素且仅在事后才访问数据库进行修复，无法从早期错误中恢复，限制了系统处理复杂分析数据库查询的能力。

Method: 提出 FlexSQL，核心设计原则是灵活数据库交互：(1) 可在推理任意时刻探索 schema 结构、检查数据值和运行验证查询；(2) 生成多样化执行计划覆盖多种查询解释；(3) 根据任务用 SQL 或 Python 实现计划；(4) 两级修复机制，可从代码级错误回溯到计划级修订。

Result: 在 Spider2-Snow 上使用 gpt-oss-120b 达到 65.4% 分数，超越使用 gpt-o3 和 DeepSeek-R1 等更强更大模型的强开源基线。集成到 Claude Code 通用编码智能体中时，带来超过 10% 的相对性能提升。消融分析表明灵活探索和灵活执行共同贡献了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 灵活性是设计高效 Text-to-SQL 系统的关键原则，通过允许智能体在整个推理过程中与数据库动态交互，能有效提升复杂分析查询的性能。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL over large analytical databases requires navigating complex schemas, resolving ambiguous queries, and grounding decisions in actual data. Most current systems follow a fixed pipeline where schema elements are retrieved once upfront and the database is only revisited for post-hoc repair, limiting recovery from early mistakes. We present FlexSQL, a text-to-SQL agent whose core design principle is flexible database interaction: the agent can explore schema structure, inspect data values, and run verification queries at any point during reasoning. FlexSQL generates diverse execution plans to cover multiple query interpretations, implements each plan in either SQL or Python depending on the task, and uses a two-tiered repair mechanism that can backtrack from code-level errors to plan-level revisions. On Spider2-Snow, using gpt-oss-120b, FlexSQL achieves a 65.4\% score, outperforming strong open-source baselines that use stronger, larger models such as gpt-o3 and DeepSeek-R1. When integrated into a general-purpose coding agent (as skills in Claude Code), our approach yields over 10\% relative improvement on Spider2-Snow. Further analysis shows that flexible exploration and flexible execution jointly contribute to the effectiveness of our approach, highlighting flexibility as a key design principle. Our code is available at: https://github.com/StringNLPLAB/FlexSQL

</details>

### [90] [2026 Roadmap on Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning for Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00839)
*Jay Lee,Hanqi Su,Marco Macchi,Adalberto Polenghi,Wei Wu,Zhiheng Zhao,George Q. Huang,Kiva Allgood,Devendra Jain,Benedikt Gieger,Vibhor Pandhare,Soumyabrata Bhattacharjee,Ram Mohril,Lingbao Kong,Qiyuan Wang,Xinlan Tang,Sungjong Kim,Chan Hee Park,Byeng D. Youn,Guo Dong Goh,Xi Huang,Wai Yee Yeong,Yung C Shin,He Zhang,Zitong Wang,Fei Tao,Jagjit Singh Srai,Satyandra K. Gupta,Byung Gun Joung,Albin John,John W. Sutherland,Sang Won Lee,Olga Fink,Vinay Sharma,Faez Ahmed,Wei Chen,Mark Fuge,Arild Waaler,Martin G. Skjæveland,Dimitris Kyritsis,Wei Chen,VispiNevile Karkaria,Yi-Ping Chen,Ying-Kuan Tsai,Joseph Cohen,Xun Huan,Jing Lin,Liangwei Zhang,Gregory W. Vogl,Aaron W. Cornelius,Xiaodong Jia,Dai-Yan Ji,Takanobu Minami,Ruoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了人工智能和机器学习在智能制造中的基础、应用与新兴方向，涵盖工业大数据分析、先进感知、自主系统、数字孪生、机器人、供应链优化及可持续制造等关键主题，并探讨了物理信息AI、生成式AI、语义AI、可解释AI、大语言模型等非传统ML方法，指出了方法、集成策略和工业采纳方面所需的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI/ML正在重塑智能制造，但其在工业部署中仍面临工业大数据复杂性、数据管理、异构系统集成以及对可信、可解释、可靠运行的需求等关键挑战，亟需系统性路线图来指导研究与产业实践。

Method: 本文采用路线图综述的形式，将内容分为三部分：第一部分阐述AI在智能制造中的基础与趋势；第二部分聚焦AI已在推动进展的关键主题；第三部分探索正在开辟新前沿的非传统ML方法，并识别各领域的机会与障碍。

Result: 识别了智能制造中AI/ML在方法、集成策略和工业采纳方面所需的进展，为研究人员、工程师和从业者提供了加速创新、对齐学术与产业优先级、实现可靠、可持续和可扩展影响的指导框架。

Conclusion: 该路线图旨在引导AI驱动的智能制造在方法创新、系统集成和工业落地方面取得突破，确保其对未来制造生态系统产生可靠、可持续和可扩展的影响。

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) is reshaping smart manufacturing by providing new capabilities for efficiency, adaptability, and autonomy across industrial value chains. However, the deployment of AI and ML in industrial settings still faces critical challenges, including the complexity of industrial big data, effective data management, integration with heterogeneous sensing and control systems, and the demand for trustworthy, explainable, and reliable operation in high-stakes industrial environments. In this roadmap, we present a comprehensive perspective on the foundations, applications, and emerging directions of AI and ML in smart manufacturing. It is structured in three parts. The first highlights the foundations and trends that frame the evolution of AI in smart manufacturing. The second focuses on key topics where AI is already enabling advances, including industrial big data analytics, advanced sensing and perception, autonomous systems, additive and laser-based manufacturing, digital twins, robotics, supply chain and logistics optimization, and sustainable manufacturing. The third section explores non-traditional ML approaches that are opening new frontiers, such as physics-informed AI, generative AI, semantic AI, advanced digital twins, explainable AI, RAMS, data-centric metrology, LLMs, and foundation models for highly connected and complex manufacturing systems. By identifying both opportunities and remaining barriers across these areas, this roadmap outlines the advances needed in methods, integration strategies, and industrial adoption. We hope this roadmap will serve as a guide for researchers, engineers, and practitioners to accelerate innovation, align academic and industrial priorities, and ensure that AI-driven smart manufacturing delivers reliable, sustainable, and scalable impact for the future of manufacturing ecosystems.

</details>


### [91] [Understanding Emergent Misalignment via Feature Superposition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00842)
*Gouki Minegishi,Hiroki Furuta,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于特征叠加几何的解释，说明微调窄领域非有害任务为何会意外增强有害行为（涌现性错位），并通过稀疏自编码器验证有害特征与诱导特征的几何邻近性，最后提出基于几何距离的过滤方法可减少34.5%的错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涌现性错位（对窄领域非有害任务的微调意外引发有害行为）是LLM安全的关键挑战，但其底层机制尚不清楚。

Method: 提出基于特征叠加的几何解释：特征以重叠表示编码，微调放大目标特征时会按相似度无意增强邻近的有害特征。给出梯度级推导，并在Gemma-2 2B/9B/27B、LLaMA-3.1 8B、GPT-OSS 20B上进行实验。使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）识别与错位诱导数据和有害行为相关的特征，并测量它们的几何距离。提出基于几何感知的训练样本过滤方法。

Result: 错位诱导特征与有害特征的几何距离显著小于非诱导特征；该趋势在健康、职业、法律建议等多个领域泛化；几何感知的过滤方法减少34.5%错位，大幅优于随机移除，与LLM-as-judge过滤效果相当或略优。

Conclusion: 涌现性错位与特征叠加现象存在因果关系，几何视角为理解和缓解该现象提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment, where fine-tuning on narrow, non-harmful tasks induces harmful behaviors, poses a key challenge for AI safety in LLMs. Despite growing empirical evidence, its underlying mechanism remains unclear. To uncover the reason behind this phenomenon, we propose a geometric account based on the geometry of feature superposition. Because features are encoded in overlapping representations, fine-tuning that amplifies a target feature also unintentionally strengthens nearby harmful features in accordance with their similarity. We give a simple gradient-level derivation of this effect and empirically test it in multiple LLMs (Gemma-2 2B/9B/27B, LLaMA-3.1 8B, GPT-OSS 20B). Using sparse autoencoders (SAEs), we identify features tied to misalignment-inducing data and to harmful behaviors, and show that they are geometrically closer to each other than features derived from non-inducing data. This trend generalizes across domains (e.g., health, career, legal advice). Finally, we show that a geometry-aware approach, filtering training samples closest to toxic features, reduces misalignment by 34.5%, substantially outperforming random removal and achieving comparable or slightly lower misalignment than LLM-as-a-judge-based filtering. Our study links emergent misalignment to feature superposition, providing a basis for understanding and mitigating this phenomenon.

</details>


### [92] [Effect-Transparent Governance for AI Workflow Architectures: Semantic Preservation, Expressive Minimality, and Decidability Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01030)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Rocq 8.19的形式化验证，证明了结构治理可以在不降低内部计算表达能力的前提下施加于AI工作流架构，并建立了七条关键性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保AI工作流架构可被有效治理（如安全约束）的同时，不牺牲其内部的计算表达能力，从而证明治理与计算表达力是正交维度。

Method: 使用Interaction Trees在Rocq 8.19中定义治理算子G，对包括内存访问、外部调用和大模型查询在内的所有带效应指令进行中介。开发包含36个模块、约12,000行代码和454条定理，零 admitted lemmas。

Result: 建立了七条性质：P1受治理的图灵完备性、P2受治理的预言机表达力、P3治理谓词的可判定性边界、P4允许执行的目标保持性、P5原始能力的表达最小性、P6结构治理严格包含内容级过滤、P7语义透明性。

Conclusion: 治理与计算表达力是正交维度：治理约束程序的效应边界，但对内部计算保持语义透明。

Abstract: We present a machine-checked formalization of structurally governed AI workflow architectures and prove that effect-level governance can be imposed without reducing internal computational expressivity. Using Interaction Trees in Rocq 8.19, we define a governance operator G that mediates all effectful directives, including memory access, external calls, and oracle (LLM) queries. Our development compiles with 0 admitted lemmas and consists of 36 modules, ~12,000 lines of Rocq, and 454 theorems. We establishseven properties: (P1) governed Turing completeness, (P2) governed oracle expressivity, (P3) a decidability boundary in which governance predicates are total and closed under Boolean composition while semantic program properties remain non-trivial and undecidable by governance, (P4) goal preservation for permitted executions, (P5) expressive minimality of primitive capabilities (compute, memory, reasoning, external call, observability), (P6) subsumption asymmetry showing structural governance strictly subsumes content-level filtering, and (P7) semantic transparency: on all executions where governance permits, the governed interpretation is observationally equivalent (modulo governance-only events) to the ungoverned interpretation. Together, these results show that governance and computational expressivity are orthogonal dimensions: governance constrains the effect boundary of programs while remaining semantically transparent to internal computation.

</details>


### [93] [Algebraic Semantics of Governed Execution: Monoidal Categories, Effect Algebras, and Coterminous Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01032)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于代数语义的受控执行框架，通过三条公理（安全性、透明性、正当性）定义 GovernanceAlgebra，构建对称幺半范畴，实现组合式治理。该框架在 Rocq 中形式化（32 个模块、~12,000 行、454 个定理、0 个 admitted），支持能力索引的组合、双保障定理，并证明治理与可表达性共延。提取的 OCaml 代码作为 NIF 在 BEAM 运行时运行，经 70,000+ 随机输入属性测试验证规范与解释器行为等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有执行治理机制缺乏代数化、组合式的形式语义支撑，难以在保证安全性的同时保持表达能力的完备性。本文旨在为受控执行提供一种公理化、组合化且与表达能力共延的代数语义基础。

Method: 基于交互树（interaction trees）和参数化余归纳（parameterized coinduction）构建 GovernanceAlgebra 记录（三条公理：安全性、透明性、正当性），诱导对称幺半范畴并验证五边形、三角形、六边形协调性；设计代数效应系统约束处理器代数，使安全片段中仅能构造保治理的处理器；通过能力索引的组合为程序附带机器检查的能力界；形式化建模共延边界和治理拒绝（安全的余归纳发散）。

Result: 在 Rocq 中完成 32 个模块、~12,000 行、454 个定理的形式化，0 个 admitted；证明能力索引组合下的 within_caps 与 gov_safe 双保障定理；证明治理与可表达性共延（共延边界定理）；Turing 完备性在治理内保持；提取的 OCaml 在 BEAM 运行时作为 NIF 通过 70,000+ 随机输入属性测试，零分歧。

Conclusion: 所提出的治理代数框架是参数化的：任何实例化三条公理的系统均可继承所有派生性质（收敛性、组合封闭性、目标保持性），为受控执行提供了坚实的代数语义基础。

Abstract: We present an algebraic semantics for governed execution in which governance is axiomatized, compositional, and coterminous with expressibility. The framework, mechanized in 32 Rocq modules (~12,000 lines, 454 theorems, 0 admitted), is built on interaction trees and parameterized coinduction. A three-axiom GovernanceAlgebra record (safety, transparency, properness) induces a symmetric monoidal category with verified pentagon, triangle, and hexagon coherence, where every tensor composition preserves governance. An algebraic effect system constrains the handler algebra so that only governance-preserving handlers can be constructed in the safe fragment; programs in the empty capability set provably emit only observability directives. Capability-indexed composition bundles programs with machine-checked capability bounds, and a dual guarantee theorem establishes that within_caps and gov_safe hold simultaneously under all composition operators. The capstone result is the coterminous boundary: within our formal model, every program expressible via the four primitive morphism constructors is governed under interpretation, and every governed program is the image of such a program. Turing completeness is preserved inside governance; unmediated I/O is excluded from the governed fragment. Governance denial is modeled as safe coinductive divergence. The governance algebra is parametric: any system instantiating the three axioms inherits all derived properties, including convergence, compositional closure, and goal preservation. Extracted OCaml runs as a NIF in the BEAM runtime, with property-based testing (70,000+ random inputs, zero disagreements) confirming behavioral equivalence between the specification and the runtime interpreter.

</details>


### [94] [A Knowledge-Driven LLM-Based Decision-Support System for Explainable Defect Analysis and Mitigation Guidance in Laser Powder Bed Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01100)
*Basit Mahmud Shahriar,Md Habibor Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识驱动的决策支持系统，将结构化缺陷知识本体与LLM推理相结合，用于增材制造（LPBF工艺）的可解释缺陷诊断与缓解指导。该系统包含27种LPBF缺陷类型的层次化知识库，支持模糊自然语言查询、多模态图像评估（基于基础模型的语义对齐评分），并通过消融实验和Cohen's kappa评估证明了本体引导的知识表示能提升LLM辅助缺陷分析的一致性、可解释性和实用性，集成配置的宏平均F1达0.808。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键的LPBF增材制造过程中，缺陷诊断和缓解指导需要可解释且可靠的知识支持，而通用大语言模型缺乏领域专业知识且解释性不足。本研究旨在通过集成结构化缺陷本体与LLM推理，构建一个兼具知识系统性、可解释性和实用性的决策支持系统。

Method: 1) 构建包含27种LPBF缺陷类型的层次化本体知识库（含因果关系的分类体系）；2) 开发LLM驱动的推理模块，支持模糊自然语言查询和文献支持的缺陷解释；3) 基于基础模型的多模态图像评估模块，通过语义对齐评分实现缺陷图像描述符引导的解读；4) 通过定性对比（vs通用视觉语言模型）、消融实验和Cohen's kappa评估者间信度分析进行系统评估。

Result: 全集成配置在文献数据集上达到0.808的宏平均F1分数，优于其他三种对照配置。Cohen's kappa分析显示模型输出与文献参考标签之间具有高度一致性（substantial agreement）。

Conclusion: 本体引导的知识表示能够提升LLM辅助LPBF缺陷分析的一致性、可解释性和实际可用性，表明结构化的领域知识与大语言模型推理相结合是提升工业决策支持系统可靠性的有效路径。

Abstract: This work presents a knowledge-driven decision-support system that integrates structured defect knowledge with LLM-based reasoning to provide explainable defect diagnosis and mitigation guidance in manufacturing, using LPBF as a representative, safety-critical case study. The proposed ontology-integrated LLM-based decision support system for LPBF defect analysis and mitigation guidance is built on a knowledge base containing 27 known LPBF defect types organized into hierarchical categories and causal relationships. The developed system supports fuzzy natural language queries for systematic knowledge retrieval, literature-supported explanation of defects, and guidance on defect causes and mitigation strategies derived from encoded process knowledge. Furthermore, a multimodal image-assessment module based on foundation models enables descriptor-guided interpretation of representative microscopic defect images through semantic alignment scoring. The proposed framework was evaluated through qualitative comparisons with general-purpose vision-language models, an ablation study, and an inter-rater reliability analysis. Evaluation on the literature-derived dataset showed that the fully integrated configuration outperformed the other three evaluated system configurations, achieving a macro-average F1 score of 0.808. Additionally, inter-rater reliability analysis using Cohen's kappa indicated substantial agreement between the model outputs and the literature-derived reference labels. These findings suggest that ontology-guided knowledge representation can improve the consistency, interpretability, and practical usefulness of LLM-assisted LPBF defect analysis.

</details>


### [95] [Virtual Speech Therapist: A Clinician-in-the-Loop AI Speech Therapy Agent for Personalized and Supervised Therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01101)
*Shakeel Sheikh,Patrick Marmaroli,MD Sahidullah,Slim Ouni,Fabrice Hirsch,Goncalo Leal,Bjorn W Schuller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VST系统，融合深度学习分类与多智能体LLM推理，实现口吃评估与个性化治疗计划的自动化生成，并通过临床专家验证其高质量与循证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口吃评估和治疗计划制定过程繁琐、依赖临床专家经验，现有方法缺乏自动化与个性化能力，临床负担重。

Method: VST采用深度学习进行口吃类型分类，随后通过多智能体LLM（含生成智能体、批判智能体）进行迭代推理，自主生成、评估并优化个性化治疗方案，最终由临床医生审核确认。

Result: 言语治疗专家的实验评估证实VST能持续生成高质量、循证的治疗建议。

Conclusion: VST系统能增强临床工作流、减轻临床医生负担，并有望改善口吃患者的治疗效果。

Abstract: This paper develops Virtual Speech Therapist (VST), an intelligent agent-based platform that streamlines stuttering assessment and delivers customized therapy planning through automated and adaptive AI-driven workflows. VST integrates state-of-the-art deep learning-based stuttering classification, and multi-agent large language model (LLM) reasoning to support evidence-based clinical decision-making. The VST begins with the acquisition and feature extraction of patient speech samples, followed by robust classification of stuttering types. Building on these outputs, VST initiates an agentic reasoning process in which specialized LLM agents autonomously generate, critique, and iteratively refine individualized therapy plans. A dedicated critic agent evaluates all generated therapy plans to ensure clinical safety, methodological soundness, and alignment with peer-reviewed evidence and established professional guidelines. The resulting output is a comprehensive, patient-specific therapy draft intended for clinician review. Incorporating clinician feedback, the system then produces a finalized therapy plan suitable for patient delivery, thereby maintaining a clinician-in-the-loop paradigm. Experimental evaluation by expert speech therapists confirms that VST consistently generates high-quality, evidence-based therapy recommendations. These findings demonstrate the system's potential to augment clinical workflows, reduce clinician burden, and improve therapeutic outcomes for individuals with speech impairments. An interactive user interface for the proposed system is available online at: https://vocametrix.com/ai/stuttering-therapy-planning-agent , facilitating real-time stuttering assessment and personalized therapy planning.

</details>


### [96] [Towards Multi-Agent Autonomous Reasoning in Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01102)
*Jinpai Zhao,Albert Cerrone,Joannes Westerink,Clint Dawson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于层级执行图（LEG）的多智能体系统（MAS）原型，用于流体动力学中的LLM驱动科学工作流。通过规划器构建查询特定的执行拓扑、专业智能体在严格工具白名单下操作、整合器融合并行输出、报告器合成最终响应，系统在37个查询上达到了93.6%的事实准确率和100%的通过率，且在高并发和数据源丢失场景下表现稳健，显著缓解了单智能体架构的上下文饱和瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体系统（SAS）在LLM驱动的科学工作流中，随着工具规范和观察轨迹的积累，有效上下文空间不断缩小，端到端可靠性下降。为缓解这一上下文饱和瓶颈，作者提出多智能体系统方案。

Method: 提出层级执行图（LEG）架构，包含：规划器（从自然语言路由启发式构建执行拓扑）、专业智能体（受严格工具白名单约束，承担互补数据类角色）、层间整合器（融合并行输出为简洁简报）、报告器（合成最终响应），运行时记录每次工具调用的来源以实现可审计性。所有基准测试均以Claude Sonnet 4.6为骨干模型。

Result: 在37个覆盖六类复杂度的查询上，达到93.6%事实准确率和100%通过率。单线程至五路并行运行中准确率保持90%以上，模拟单个数据源丢失时系统仍能降级返回实质性部分答案。

Conclusion: 规划器引导的图结构多智能体编排能够有效缓解单智能体架构的上下文饱和瓶颈，在科学工作流中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Single-agent systems (SAS) have become the default pattern for LLM-driven scientific workflows, but routing planning, tool use, and synthesis through a single context window comes with a well-known cost: as tool specifications and observational traces accumulate, the effective context available for each decision shrinks, and end-to-end reliability suffers. We present a multi-agent system (MAS) prototype for hydrodynamics in which specialized agents are coordinated through a Layer Execution Graph (LEG). A planner agent constructs query-specific execution topologies from natural-language routing heuristics that capture domain knowledge without hard-coding it as rigid control logic; specialist agents operate under strict tool allowlists and occupy complementary data-class roles. Between layers, consolidator agents fuse parallel outputs into concise briefs, and a reporter agent synthesizes the final response, while the runtime logs provenance for every tool invocation to support auditability. All benchmarks, ablations, and stress tests use Claude Sonnet~4.6 as the backbone model for both specialist and general-purpose agents. Evaluated on 37 queries spanning six complexity categories, the prototype achieves 93.6% factual precision with a 100% pass rate. Accuracy remains above 90% across runs from single-threaded to five independent parallel tracks, and under simulated loss of individual data sources the system degrades gracefully, still returning substantive partial answers. Together, these results suggest that planner-guided, graph-structured multi-agent orchestration can meaningfully alleviate the context-saturation bottlenecks that constrain monolithic single-agent architectures.

</details>


### [97] [New Bounds for Zarankiewicz Numbers via Reinforced LLM Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01120)
*Jay Bhan,Nicole Nobili,Srinivasan Raghuraman,Patrick Langer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次确定了三个Zarankiewicz数的精确值：Z(11,21,3,3)=116、Z(11,22,3,3)=121、Z(12,22,3,3)=132，并为41个更多Zarankiewicz数建立了下界，其中多个与已知最优上界仅差一条边。这些结果通过基于大语言模型(LLM)的进化算法OpenEvolve获得，每个参数组合的计算成本不到30美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zarankiewicz数是极值图论中的经典问题，确定其精确值长期具有挑战性。本文旨在利用基于大语言模型的进化搜索算法，以低成本、可重复的方式为尚未解决的Zarankiewicz数提供新的构造和精确值。

Method: 采用OpenEvolve，一个基于大语言模型(LLM)的开源进化算法，通过优化针对该问题定制的奖励信号，迭代改进用于生成数学构造的算法。该方法以极低的计算成本（每个参数组合不到30美元）搜索新的二部图构造。

Result: 首次确定了三个Zarankiewicz数的精确值(Z(11,21,3,3)=116, Z(11,22,3,3)=121, Z(12,22,3,3)=132)；为41个更多Zarankiewicz数建立了下界，其中多个与已知最优上界仅差一条边；在四个更多封闭情形中匹配了已确立的值。

Conclusion: LLM引导的进化搜索是一种廉价、可重复且易于使用的工具，能够为组合数学发现提供新的极值图构造，展示了AI在数学研究中的潜力。

Abstract: The Zarankiewicz number $\textbf{Z}(m, n, s, t)$ is the maximum number of edges in a bipartite graph $G_{m, n}$ such that there is no complete $K_{s, t}$ bipartite subgraph. We determine for the first time the exact values of three Zarankiewicz numbers: $\textbf{Z}(11, 21, 3, 3)=116$, $\textbf{Z}(11, 22, 3, 3)=121$, and $\textbf{Z}(12, 22, 3, 3)=132$. We further establish lower bounds for 41 more Zarankiewicz numbers, including several that are within one edge of the best known upper bound, and we match the established value in four more closed cases. Our results are obtained using OpenEvolve, an open-source evolutionary algorithm based on Large Language Models (LLMs) that iteratively improves algorithms for generating mathematical constructions by optimizing a reward signal which we tailored for this specific problem. These findings provide new extremal graph constructions and demonstrate the potential of LLM-guided evolutionary search to contribute to mathematical research. In addition to presenting the resulting constructions, we report the generation algorithms produced, describe the relevant implementation details, and provide our computational costs. Our costs are remarkably low, at less than \$30 for each Zarankiewicz parameter combination, showing that LLM-guided evolutionary search can be an inexpensive, reproducible, and accessible tool for discovering new combinatorial constructions.

</details>


### [98] [Iterative Finetuning is Mostly Idempotent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01130)
*Zephaniah Roe,Jack Sanderson,Dang Nguyen,Julian Huang,Todd Nief,Aryan Shrivastava,Chenhao Tan,Ari Holtzman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究模型在自身输出上训练时，行为倾向（如谄媚、失调）是否会在下一代模型中被放大。实验覆盖SFT、SDF和DPO三种微调方式，发现SFT/SDF中特质大多衰减或保持不变，DPO中特质放大可在持续训练时可靠发生但可通过重新初始化消除，且放大与连贯性存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究模型在自身生成数据上进行迭代训练时，其固有的行为倾向（如谄媚、失调）是否会被逐代放大，从而引发对齐风险。

Method: 通过系列实验：初始模型植入特定人格/信念，然后在其自身生成数据上依次微调下一代模型。在三个设置中测试：监督微调（SFT）、合成文档微调（SDF）和直接偏好优化（DPO）。

Result: SFT和SDF设置中，特质大多衰减或保持不变，极少出现放大且常伴随连贯性下降；DPO设置中，当模型持续用偏好自身输出训练时，特质放大可靠发生，但每轮重新初始化模型后可消除。

Conclusion: 特质放大主要源自持续性后训练（特别是DPO式训练），限制该阶段可有效防御；非RL微调中特质放大罕见且对数据量敏感，不易偶然发生；放大-连贯性权衡本身可作为自然制约。

Abstract: If a model has some behavioral tendency, such as sycophancy or misalignment, and it is trained on its own outputs, will the tendency be amplified in the next generation of models? We study this question by training a series of models where each model is finetuned on data generated by its predecessor, and the initial model is seeded with some persona or belief. We test three settings: supervised finetuning (SFT) on instruct models, synthetic document finetuning (SDF) on base models, and direct preference optimization (DPO). In the SFT and SDF settings, traits mostly decay or remain constant so that further finetuning cycles do nothing. In rare cases when amplification occurs, it generally comes at the cost of coherence. In the DPO setting, trait amplification can reliably occur when a model is continually trained with a preference for its own outputs, but vanishes when models are reinitialized at each cycle. Overall, our results suggest that amplification most likely comes from continual post-training, and limiting this stage may be an effective defense. For non-RL finetuning, trait amplification is rare and very sensitive to data quantity, making it significantly less likely to occur accidentally. Finally, the amplification-coherence tradeoff serves as a natural deterrent against trait amplification.

</details>


### [99] [To Use AI as Dice of Possibilities with Timing Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01134)
*Jia Li,Vipin Kumar,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动词的AI建模范式，通过时间计算和可能性的精确定义，使AI能够表达开放的时间维度。在3276名乳腺癌患者的纵向EHR数据上，该方法实现了临床显著患者轨迹的自动发现和反事实时间推演，均为纯数据驱动且无需领域知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以名词为基础的建模范式从根本上限制了AI发展，无法充分表征未来作为一个开放的时间维度。

Method: 引入基于动词的范式，给出时间计算和可能性的精确定义。在3276名乳腺癌患者的纵向电子健康记录数据上进行实证。

Result: (1) 自动发现具有临床意义的患者轨迹；(2) 反事实时间推演。两项结果均为纯数据驱动，无需先验领域知识。

Conclusion: 该框架使AI能够成为实现思维语法的有效工具，代表了机器学习文献中首次此类演示。

Abstract: The dominant noun-based modeling paradigm has fundamentally constrained AI development, precluding any adequate representation of the future as an open temporal dimension. This paper introduces a verb-based paradigm, together with precise definitions of \emph{timing computation} and \emph{possibility}, that enables AI to function as an effective instrument for realizing the grammar of our thought.
  Applied to longitudinal EHR data from 3,276 breast cancer patients, the framework empirically demonstrates: (1) automatic discovery of clinically significant patient trajectories, and (2) counterfactual timing deduction. Both results are purely data-driven, require no prior domain knowledge, and, to our knowledge, represent the first such demonstrations in the machine learning literature.

</details>


### [100] [A Low-Latency Fraud Detection Layer for Detecting Adversarial Interaction Patterns in LLM-Powered Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01143)
*Sheldon Yu,Yingcheng Sun,Hanqing Guo,Julian McAuley,Qianqian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于LLM智能体的低延迟欺诈检测层，通过结构化运行时特征对交互轨迹进行风险建模，使用42维特征和XGBoost分类器实现比LLM检测器快9倍以上的实时检测，填补了现有提示级过滤和规则防护在渐进式风险场景下的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的自主性引入新的攻击面（直接提示注入、间接内容攻击、多轮升级策略），现有基于提示级过滤和规则防护的防御策略无法有效应对在交互序列中渐进出现的风险。

Method: 提出一个低延迟欺诈检测层，利用来自提示特征、会话动态、工具使用、执行上下文的42个结构化运行时特征，采用XGBoost分类器对多轮交互轨迹进行风险建模，构建了12,000个多轮智能体交互的合成语料库用于评估。

Result: 检测器基于XGBoost和42个结构化特征，实现比LLM检测器快9倍以上的速度，在合成语料库上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 交互级行为检测应成为LLM智能体部署时防御的核心组件，该检测层可补充现有防御策略，实现低延迟实时部署。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-powered agents demonstrate strong capabilities in autonomous task execution, tool use, and multi-step reasoning. However, their increasing autonomy also introduces a new attack surface: adversarial interactions can manipulate agent behavior through direct prompt injection, indirect content attacks, and multi-turn escalation strategies. Existing defense strategies focus on prompt-level filtering and rule-based guardrails, which are often insufficient when risk emerges gradually across interaction sequences. In this work, we propose a complementary defense mechanism: a low-latency fraud detection layer for detecting adversarial interaction patterns in LLM-powered agents. Instead of determining whether a single prompt is malicious, our approach models risk over interaction trajectories using structured runtime features derived from prompt characteristics, session dynamics, tool usage, execution context, and fraud-inspired signals. The detection layer can be implemented using lightweight models leading to low-latency real-time deployments. To evaluate the framework, we construct a synthetic corpus of 12,000 multi-turn agent interactions generated from parameterized templates that simulate realistic agentic workflows. Using 42 structured features and an XGBoost classifier, our detector achieves over 9 times faster than LLM-based detectors. Through the experiment and ablation studies, our work suggests that interaction-level behavioral detection should become a core component of deployment-time defense for LLM-powered agents.

</details>


### [101] [Position: Safety and Fairness in Agentic AI Depend on Interaction Topology, Not on Model Scale or Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01147)
*Tanav Singh Bajaj,Nikhil Singh,Karan Anand,Eishkaran Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了多智能体AI系统的安全性主要由交互拓扑结构决定，而非单个模型的权重或对齐程度。顺序推理、并行投票等拓扑结构会导致排序不稳定性、信息级联和功能崩溃三类病态现象，且更强模型会加剧这些问题。因此智能体AI应被视为动力系统，交互拓扑必须成为安全评估和监管的主要目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全社区假设个体模型的安全属性可以组合成安全的群体行为，本文认为这一假设从根本上就是错误的，亟需纠正。

Method: 通过跨模型族和规模的经验证据，分析不同交互拓扑（顺序推理、并行投票+法官）下涌现的三种病态现象，论证拓扑结构对安全性的主导作用。

Result: 发现三类拓扑驱动的病态：排序不稳定性（系统行为主要取决于智能体顺序）、信息级联（早期判断无论正误都会传播）、功能崩溃（系统在满足公平性指标的同时放弃有意义的风险区分）。更强的模型会通过增强共识形成来强化这些效应。

Conclusion: 智能体AI必须被视为动力系统而非对齐组件的集合；交互拓扑应成为安全评估和监管的主要目标，系统在部署前需证明对架构变化具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed as interacting agents in high-stakes decisions, the AI safety community assumes that safety properties of individual models will compose into safe multi-agent behavior. This position paper argues that this assumption is fundamentally mistaken. In agentic AI, safety is determined by interaction topology, not model weights. When agents deliberate sequentially or aggregate via parallel voting with a judge, the structure of information flow and decision coupling dominates outcomes. Evidence across model families and scales reveals three persistent topology-driven pathologies: ordering instability, where system behavior depends primarily on agent sequence; information cascades, where early judgments propagate regardless of correctness; and functional collapse, where systems satisfy fairness metrics while abandoning meaningful risk discrimination. Contrary to intuition, scaling to more capable models strengthens these effects by increasing consensus formation and reducing the challenge of initial decisions. These failure modes are invisible to model-centric evaluation and alignment procedures. We argue that agentic AI must be treated as a dynamical system rather than a collection of aligned components. Interaction topology must become a primary target of safety evaluation and regulation, with systems required to demonstrate robustness across architectural variations before deployment.

</details>


### [102] [Arithmetic in the Wild: Llama uses Base-10 Addition to Reason About Cyclic Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01148)
*Sheridan Feucht,Tal Haklay,Usha Bhalla,Daniel Wurgaft,Can Rager,Raphaël Sarfati,Jack Merullo,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Llama-3.1-8B在处理循环概念（如月份）时，并非直接进行模加运算，而是先使用基数为10的加法计算和，再将结果映射回循环空间。该过程复用了与任务无关的傅里叶特征（周期为2、5、10等），并由第18层中约0.2%的MLP神经元（共28个）实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型中的结构表示是否必然导致结构化的计算过程，通过研究模型如何处理具有循环结构的概念（如月份、方向等）来揭示表示与计算之间的关系。

Method: 以Llama-3.1-8B为研究对象，分析其在循环概念（如"八月之后六个月是几月"）上的推理机制。通过因果抽象和特征几何分析，识别模型中使用的傅里叶特征及其周期，并定位执行这些计算的特定MLP神经元。

Result: 模型并非直接进行周期为12的模加运算，而是先进行基数为10的标准加法（如6+8=14），再映射回循环概念空间（14→二月）。使用的傅里叶特征周期为2、5、10等（与十进制加法一致），而非概念本身的周期（如12）。在第18层发现28个MLP神经元（约0.2%）被复用于所有任务，可划分为多个不相交的簇，每个簇计算不同周期傅里叶特征的和。

Conclusion: 语言模型中的循环表示并不一定对应循环计算，模型可能复用通用的、任务无关的算术机制。因果抽象与特征几何的结合能有效加深对语言模型内部机制的理解。

Abstract: Does structure in representations imply structure in computation? We study how Llama-3.1-8B reasons over cyclic concepts (e.g., "what month is six months after August?"). Even though Llama-3.1-8B's representations for these concepts are circularly structured, we find that instead of directly computing modular addition in the period of the cyclic concept (e.g., 12 for months), the model re-uses a generic addition mechanism across tasks that operates independently of concept-specific geometry. First, it computes the sum of its two inputs using base-10 addition (six + August=14). Then, it maps this sum back to cyclic concept space (14->February). We show that Llama-3.1-8B uses task-agnostic Fourier features to compute these sums--in fact, these features have periods that respect standard base-10 addition, e.g., 2, 5, and 10, rather than the cyclic concept period (e.g., 12 for months). Furthermore, we identify a sparse set of 28 MLP neurons re-used across all tasks (approximately 0.2% of the MLP at layer 18) that can be partitioned into disjoint clusters, each computing the sum for a Fourier feature with a different period. Our work highlights how an interplay between causal abstraction and feature geometry can deepen our mechanistic understanding of LMs.

</details>


### [103] [LLMs Should Not Yet Be Credited with Decision Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01164)
*Wenshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为LLMs尚不应被赋予决策解释（decision explanation）的信任，区分了决策预测、理由生成和决策解释三种主张，提出了决策解释的信誉标准桥梁，并主张信誉校准原则：LLMs应获得其证据所能支持的最强主张的信誉，而非更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作越来越多地将准确的行为预测、合理的理由阐述和基于结果的推理轨迹视为LLMs能够解释人们为何做出特定决策的证据，这可能导致对人类决策建模中何为解释性进展的过早重新定义，存在误导风险。

Method: 首先区分三种不同举证负担的主张（决策预测、理由生成、决策解释）；其次论证常见证据仅支持前两种主张及假设生成，无法区分解释与合理化；进而提出决策解释信誉的桥梁标准（明确解释目标、排除弱合理化替代、采用过程或干预敏感验证、限定范围）；最后与竞争观点和相关文献进行对比定位。

Result: 明确了当前LLM决策相关证据无法支撑决策解释这一更强的主张，提出了可操作的评估标准来区分真正的解释与仅具预测支持性的合理化。

Conclusion: 应采纳信誉校准原则：LLMs应获得其证据所能支持的最强主张的信誉，而非更强。这有助于将LLMs从有说服力的决策叙述者转变为发现、检验和沟通人类行为解释的更可靠工具。

Abstract: This position paper argues that LLMs should not yet be credited with decision explanation. This matters because recent work increasingly treats accurate behavioral prediction, plausible rationales, and outcome-conditioned reasoning traces as evidence that LLMs explain why people decide as they do, risking a premature redefinition of what counts as explanatory progress in human decision modeling. We first distinguish three claims with different evidential burdens: decision prediction, rationale generation, and decision explanation. We then argue that the evidence most commonly offered for LLM-based decision accounts directly supports the first two claims, and sometimes explanatory hypothesis generation, but does not distinguish decision explanation from prediction-supportive rationalization. Next, we propose a bridge standard for decision-explanation credit: stronger claims should specify explanatory targets, discriminate against weaker rationalizer alternatives, use target-appropriate process- or intervention-sensitive validation, and bound their scope. We then situate this standard against competing views and related literatures, clarifying why it preserves the value of LLMs as predictors, narrators, and hypothesis generators while resisting premature explanatory credit. We conclude with a principle of credit calibration: LLMs should be credited for the strongest claim their evidence warrants, and no stronger; if adopted, this principle can help turn LLMs from persuasive narrators of decisions into more reliable instruments for discovering, testing, and communicating explanations of human behavior.

</details>


### [104] [NEURON: A Neuro-symbolic System for Grounded Clinical Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01189)
*Anuradha Chandrasekaran,Dimitrios Zikos,Mutlu Mete,Alan Pang,Brady D. Lund,Kewei Sha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NEURON是一个神经符号系统，通过整合SNOMED CT本体知识图谱、机器学习模型和RAG增强的LLM解释层，在MIMIC-IV急性心力衰竭死亡率预测任务中将AUC从0.74-0.77提升至0.84-0.88，并在临床可解释性上显著优于原始SHAP可视化（0.85 vs 0.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服临床AI部署中高性能模型缺乏本体论基础和叙事透明度的黑箱/灰箱问题，提供专业级的可解释性。

Method: NEURON系统包含三个核心组件：(1) SNOMED CT本体驱动的结构化表示，桥接原始数据与医学命名法；(2) 机器学习模型进行预测；(3) RAG增强的LLM层，将SHAP特征归因与患者临床笔记合成为自然语言解释。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV急性心力衰竭死亡率预测中，AUC从0.74-0.77提升至0.84-0.88；在人机对齐指标上，NEURON达0.85，显著优于原始SHAP可视化的0.50。

Conclusion: NEURON提供了一个鲁棒、可扩展的工程解决方案，用于部署可信赖、以人为中心的互联健康应用。

Abstract: Clinical AI adoption is hindered by the black-box/grey-box nature of high-performing models, which lack the ontological grounding and narrative transparency required for professional-level explainability. We present NEURON, a neuro-symbolic system designed to enhance both predictive reliability and clinical interpretability. NEURON integrates SNOMED CT ontology-informed structural representations with machine learning models to bridge the gap between raw data and medical nomenclature. To facilitate human-aligned interaction, the system utilizes a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounded LLM layer to synthesize SHAP feature attributions and patient-specific clinical notes into coherent, natural-language explanations. Validated on the MIMIC-IV dataset for Acute Heart Failure mortality prediction, NEURON improved the AUC from 0.74-0.77 to 0.84-0.88 and significantly outperformed raw SHAP visualizations in human-aligned metrics (0.85 vs. 0.50). Our results demonstrate that NEURON offers a robust, scalable engineering solution for deploying trustworthy, human-centered connected health applications.

</details>


### [105] [GR-Ben: A General Reasoning Benchmark for Evaluating Process Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01203)
*Zhouhao Sun,Xuan Zhang,Xiao Ding,Bibo Cai,Li Du,Kai Xiong,Xinran Dai,Fei Zhang,weidi tang,Zhiyuan Kan,Yang Zhao,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GR-Ben，一个用于评估过程奖励模型（PRM）在科学和逻辑两大推理领域（含9个子域）中错误检测能力的基准。实验表明，PRM和LLM在数学以外的错误检测能力显著较弱，且PRM不擅长识别知识错误，LLM不擅长检测计算错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRM基准主要聚焦数学推理，无法全面评估PRM在多样化推理场景下的错误检测能力，亟需一个覆盖更广推理领域的评估基准。

Method: 构建GR-Ben基准，涵盖科学和逻辑两大推理领域、9个子域，并在22个模型（包括PRM和LLM）上进行大规模实验。

Result: (1) 在数学之外的领域，现有PRM和LLM的错误检测能力明显更弱；(2) PRM更不擅长识别知识型错误，LLM更不擅长检测计算型错误。

Conclusion: GR-Ben可促进通用领域PRM研究，从而增强LLM的推理能力。

Abstract: Currently, process reward models (PRMs) have exhibited remarkable potential for test-time scaling. Since large language models (LLMs) regularly generate flawed intermediate reasoning steps when tackling a broad spectrum of reasoning and decision-making tasks, PRMs are required to possess capabilities for detecting process-level errors in real-world scenarios. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on mathematical reasoning, thereby failing to comprehensively evaluate the error detection ability of PRMs across diverse reasoning scenarios. To mitigate this gap, we introduce GR-Ben, a process-level benchmark specifically designed for assessing PRM's performance across two primary reasoning domains (science and logic) and nine subdomains. We conduct extensive experiments on a diverse set of 22 models, encompassing both PRMs and LLMs, and derive two key findings: (1) In domains beyond mathematical reasoning, the error-detection ability of existing PRMs and LLMs is found to be markedly weaker by comparison.(2) In general, PRMs are less adept at identifying knowledge-based errors, whereas LLMs exhibit poorer performance in detecting computational errors.We hope GR-Ben can foster future researches on PRMs for general domains, thereby enhancing the reasoning capabilities of LLMs.

</details>


### [106] [Faithful Mobile GUI Agents with Guided Advantage Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01208)
*Haowen Hu,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Lingzhong Dong,Gongshen Liu,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Faithful-Agent，一个以忠实性优先的 GUI 智能体框架，通过两阶段流水线（忠实性导向的 SFT + 基于引导优势估计器 GuAE 的 RFT）解决视觉-语言模型在 GUI 交互中依赖记忆捷径而非屏幕证据和用户指令的不忠实行为，将陷阱成功率从 13.88% 提升至 80.21%，同时保持通用指令跟随性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型驱动的 GUI 智能体虽然具备强交互能力，但常常行为不忠实，即依赖记忆化的捷径而非基于屏幕显示证据或用户指令进行行动。作者旨在解决这一忠实性问题，确保智能体的行为真正 grounded 在视觉证据上。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(i) 忠实性导向的 SFT 阶段——在证据扰动下灌输放弃行为（abstainment behaviors）；(ii) RFT 阶段——引入引导优势估计器（GuAE），这是一种基于锚点且方差自适应的优势调节机制，构建于 GRPO 之上，防止稀疏 GUI 奖励下低方差 rollout 组中的优势崩溃，并结合思想-行动一致性奖励。

Result: 相比基线，Faithful-Agent（第二阶段）将陷阱成功率（Trap SR）从 13.88% 提升至 80.21%，同时保持稳健的通用指令跟随性能。

Conclusion: 通过强调证据基础性和内部一致性的忠实性优先框架，能够显著提升 GUI 智能体在对抗性设置下的可靠性，同时不牺牲通用任务完成能力。

Abstract: Vision-language model based graphical user interface (GUI) agents have shown strong interaction capabilities. However, they often behave unfaithfully, relying on memorized shortcuts rather than grounding actions in displayed screen evidence or user instructions. To address this, we propose Faithful-Agent, a faithfulness-first framework that reformulates GUI interaction to prioritize evidence groundedness and internal consistency. Faithful-Agent employs a two-stage pipeline: (i) a faithfulness-oriented SFT stage to instill abstainment behaviors under evidence perturbations; (ii) an RFT stage that further amplifies faithfulness by introducing the guided advantage estimator (GuAE), an anchor-based and variance-adaptive advantage tempering mechanism built upon GRPO. GuAE prevents advantage collapse in low-variance rollout groups under sparse GUI rewards, and with a thought-action consistency reward, Faithful-Agent (Stage II) elevates the Trap SR from 13.88\% to 80.21\% relative to the baseline, while preserving robust general instruction-following performance.

</details>


### [107] [Zero-Shot Signal Temporal Logic Planning with Disjunctive Branch Selection in Dynamic Semantic Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01222)
*Bowen Ye,Ancheng Hou,Junyue Huang,Ruijia Liu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种零样本STL规划求解器，结合地图条件Transformer和轻量级启发式方法，利用传递强化学习处理复杂析取子公式，实现对变化环境的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL规划面临挑战：精确优化方法过慢，学习方法难以泛化到不同环境，亟需一种无需重新训练即可适应变化环境的零样本求解器。

Method: 集成地图条件Transformer架构处理变图环境，结合轻量级启发式处理复杂析取子公式，利用传递强化学习(TRL)确保子任务间的时间基础一致性和逻辑连贯性。

Result: 在含多样障碍物布局的动态语义地图上实验，展示了框架在变化环境中的零样本泛化能力和广泛的STL覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 提出的零样本STL规划求解器能有效应对变化环境，无需重新训练即可生成可行轨迹，且具备良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) offers verifiable task specifications and is crucial for safety-critical control. Yet STL planning remains challenging: exact optimization-based methods are often too slow, and learning-based methods struggle to generalize across varying environments. We propose a zero-shot STL planning solver for variable-map environments that generates feasible trajectories without retraining. By integrating a map-conditioned Transformer architecture with a lightweight heuristic, our approach effectively handles complex disjunctive (OR) subformulas. Furthermore, we leverage Transitive Reinforcement Learning (TRL) to ensure consistent temporal grounding and logical coherence across decomposed sub-tasks. Experiments on dynamic semantic maps with diverse obstacle layouts demonstrate consistent gains, highlighting the framework's superior zero-shot generalization to changing environments and broad STL coverage.

</details>


### [108] [EO-Gym: A Multimodal, Interactive Environment for Earth Observation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01250)
*Sai Ma,Zhuang Li,Sichao Li,Xinyue Xu,Ruibiao Zhu,Tony Boston,John A. Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EO-Gym是一个面向地球观测交互式分析的框架和基准测试，包含66万+多模态文件、35个专业化工具和9,078条轨迹数据。实验表明，通用视觉语言模型在交互式EO推理上表现不佳，而通过微调得到的EO-Gym-4B模型将Pass@3从0.49提升至0.74。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EO基准测试将分析过程简化为固定输入的单轮任务，忽略了EO分析固有的交互性（如扩展感兴趣区域、检索历史观测、切换传感器模态等），缺乏支持多模态、工具化交互式EO智能体的可执行框架。

Method: 构建了EO-Gym框架，包括：1) 基于Gymnasium风格的局部地理空间工作区，索引超过66万份多模态文件；2) 35个覆盖6大任务族的EO专用工具；3) EO-Gym-Data基准（9,078条轨迹，34,604个推理步骤），基于8个公开EO数据集及Landsat/Sentinel-2影像构建。在此基础上，对Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct微调得到EO-Gym-4B基线模型。

Result: 10个开源和闭源VLM评估显示，通用模型在交互式EO推理上表现不佳，特别是在时空和跨模态工作流上。EO-Gym-4B在主要评估设置下将Pass@3从0.49提升至0.74。

Conclusion: EO-Gym为交互式EO智能体提供了可复现的环境，将EO分析形式化为需要跨地理空间、时间和传感模态规划的取证问题。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) analysis is inherently interactive: resolving uncertainty often requires expanding the region of interest, retrieving historical observations, and switching across sensors such as optical and Synthetic Aperture Radar. However, most EO benchmarks collapse this process into fixed-input, single-turn tasks. To address this gap, we present EO-Gym, a controlled executable framework for multimodal, tool-using EO agents that formulates EO analysis as a Gymnasium-style local geospatial workspace backed by more than 660k multimodal files indexed by location, time, and sensor type, with 35 EO-specialized tools spanning six task families. Built on this environment, we construct EO-Gym-Data, a benchmark of 9,078 trajectories and 34,604 reasoning steps, and grounded in eight public EO datasets together with Landsat and Sentinel-2 imagery. Evaluating $10$ open and closed VLMs shows that strong general-purpose models still struggle with interactive EO reasoning, especially on temporal and cross-modal workflows. As a reference baseline, EO-Gym-4B, obtained by fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct on EO-Gym-Data, improves overall Pass@3 from $0.49$ to $0.74$ under the main evaluation setting. O-Gym provides a reproducible environment for interactive EO agents, operationalizing EO as an evidence-gathering problem that requires planning across geospatial, temporal, and sensing modality.

</details>


### [109] [Valley3: Scaling Omni Foundation Models for E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01278)
*Zeyu Chen,Guanghao Zhou,Qixiang Yin,Ziwang Zhao,Huanjin Yao,Pengjiu Xia,Min Yang,Cen Chen,Minghui Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Valley3，一个面向全球电商场景的全模态大语言模型（MLLM），具备文本、图像、视频、音频的统一理解和推理能力。通过四阶段全模态电商连续预训练流程，模型逐步获得音频理解、跨模态指令跟随、电商领域知识及长上下文推理能力。后训练阶段引入可控推理模式（非思考模式+三级思考），平衡简单场景的效率与复杂应用的深度推理。同时配备智能搜索能力以支持电商深度研究。实验表明，Valley3在自建和开源电商基准上持续优于强基线，在通用域基准上也保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在电商场景中对音频（尤其是短视频场景中的多语言音频）支持不足，缺乏统一的全模态电商理解和推理能力。现有模型也难以兼顾简单场景的推理效率与复杂应用的长链推理需求，且在电商深度研究任务中缺乏自主信息检索能力。

Method: 1) 四阶段全模态电商持续预训练：依次训练音频理解、跨模态指令跟随、电商领域知识、长上下文推理。2) 后训练：引入可控推理模式，包括非思考模式和三级思考模式（逐步增加推理深度）。3) 配备自主搜索工具以支持电商深度研究。4) 构建涵盖6个任务的全模态电商评测基准。

Result: Valley3在自建和开源电商基准上持续优于强基线模型，在通用域基准上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的四阶段预训练、可控推理模式后训练以及智能搜索能力，Valley3在电商全模态任务上取得了SOTA性能，有效解决了音频理解不足、推理效率与深度平衡、信息检索等问题，验证了面向电商场景的全模态大语言模型的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we present Valley3, an omni multimodal large language model (MLLM) developed for diverse global e-commerce tasks, with unified understanding and reasoning capabilities across text, images, video, and audio. A key feature of Valley3 is its native multilingual audio capability for e-commerce, developed by extending vision-language models to better support crucial audio-visual tasks, particularly in short-video scenarios. To achieve this, we carefully design a four-stage omni e-commerce continued pre-training pipeline, through which Valley3 progressively acquires audio understanding, cross-modal instruction-following, e-commerce domain knowledge, and long-context reasoning capabilities, ultimately evolving into an omni model for diverse e-commerce scenarios. Then, we further improve Valley3 through post-training to encourage long-chain reasoning with controllable reasoning modes, enabling one non-thinking mode and three distinct levels of thinking, thereby balancing inference efficiency in simple scenarios with deep reasoning for complex applications. Moreover, we equip Valley3 with agentic search capabilities to proactively invoke search tools and acquire task-relevant information for e-commerce deep research tasks. To comprehensively assess the capabilities of Valley3, we construct an omni e-commerce benchmark spanning 6 tasks. Experimental results show that Valley3 consistently outperforms strong baselines on our in-house and open-source e-commerce benchmarks, while remaining competitive on general-domain benchmarks.

</details>


### [110] [Lifting Traces to Logic: Programmatic Skill Induction with Neuro-Symbolic Learning for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01293)
*Jie-Jing Shao,Haiyan Yin,Yueming Lyu,Xingrui Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo,Ivor Tsang,James Kwok,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NSI是一个神经符号技能归纳框架，通过将交互轨迹转化为带逻辑基础、含显式控制流和动态变量绑定的模块化程序，使智能体能够发现何时及为何行动，从而在长时域规划任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的智能体在长时域规划中表现不佳，因为纯提示推理具有瞬时性；现有技能归纳方法虽能蒸馏经验，但生成的状态盲参数化脚本缺乏条件逻辑，无法在动态环境中稳健执行。

Method: 提出神经符号技能归纳（NSI）框架，将交互轨迹提升为模块化的、基于逻辑的程序，通过合成显式控制流和动态变量绑定，使智能体发现何时（when）及为何（why）采取行动。

Result: 在一系列智能体任务中，NSI consistently优于最先进基线方法，使智能体能够从少样本示例中归纳技能并灵活适应未见过的目标。

Conclusion: NSI范式使智能体能够自我进化为逻辑基础技能的架构师，实现了高效泛化。

Abstract: Foundation model-driven agents often struggle with long-horizon planning due to the transient nature of purely prompting-based reasoning. While existing skill induction methods mitigate this by distilling experience into state-blind parameterized scripts, they fail to capture the conditional logic required for robust execution in dynamic environments. In this paper, we propose Neuro-Symbolic Skill Induction (NSI), a framework that lifts interaction traces into modular, \textit{logic-grounded} programs. By synthesizing explicit control flows and dynamic variable binding, NSI empowers agents to discover \textit{when} and \textit{why} to act. This paradigm enables the efficient generalization, allowing agents to induce skills from few-shot examples and flexibly adapt to unseen goals. Experiments on a series of agentic tasks demonstrate that NSI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, empowering agents to self-evolve into architects of logic-grounded skills.

</details>


### [111] [Segment-Aligned Policy Optimization for Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01327)
*Lei Gao,Zhuoming Li,Mengxi Jia,Jiakang Yuan,Hongbo Sun,Hao Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Segment-Aligned Policy Optimization (SAPO)，一种以推理步骤为基本单元进行策略优化的强化学习范式，在推理任务上优于token级和序列级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM强化学习方法以单个token或整句序列为粒度进行策略优化，与推理过程的自然步骤结构不匹配，导致多模态推理任务中信用分配欠佳、训练不稳定。

Method: SAPO将推理片段作为策略更新的基本单元，引入步骤级马尔可夫决策过程抽象，配套片段级价值估计、优势计算和重要性采样机制，与推理边界语义对齐。

Result: 在代表性推理基准上，SAPO一致优于token级和序列级策略优化方法，取得显著准确率提升，同时表现出更好的训练稳定性和价值估计一致性。

Conclusion: 将强化学习更新与推理的内在结构对齐至关重要，SAPO为复杂推理任务中更高效、语义更扎实的策略优化提供新方向。

Abstract: Existing reinforcement learning approaches for Large Language Models typically perform policy optimization at the granularity of individual tokens or entire response sequences. However, such formulations often misalign with the natural step-wise structure of reasoning processes, leading to suboptimal credit assignment and unstable training in multi-modal reasoning tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose Segment-Aligned Policy Optimization (SAPO), a novel reinforcement learning paradigm that treats coherent reasoning steps, rather than tokens or full sequences as fundamental units of policy update.
