### [185] [HAAS: A Policy-Aware Framework for Adaptive Task Allocation Between Humans and Artificial Intelligence Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02832)
*Vicente Pelechanoa,Antoni Mestre,Manoli Albert,Miriam Gil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HAAS框架，一种结合规则专家系统与上下文强盗学习器的自适应人机任务分配方法，在软件工程和制造领域验证了治理是可调设计变量、适度的治理能产生工作负载缓冲效应，且不同情境下最优治理策略不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机任务分配通常被视为二元选择，但实际运作中人类与AI常共享任务或互补协作，如何平衡效率、监督与人类能力仍是一个开放问题。

Method: HAAS框架包含两个耦合组件：规则专家系统（学习前强制执行治理约束）和上下文强盗学习器（根据结果反馈选择协作模式）。通过五个可审计认知维度和五级自主性光谱表示任务-智能体匹配度，并在软件工程和制造领域建立可复现基准。

Result: 三个实证发现：(1)治理是可调设计变量，更严格约束会将自主AI分配转为监督协作；(2)制造领域中更强治理可同时提升运营绩效并减轻疲劳（工作负载缓冲效应）；(3)不同情境下最优治理策略不同，适度治理随着学习器经验积累而更具竞争力。

Conclusion: HAAS可作为部署前的工作台，用于比较和检查人机分配策略，为组织决策提供依据。

Abstract: Deciding how to distribute work between humans and AI systems is a central challenge in organisational design. Most approaches treat this as a binary choice, yet the operational reality is richer: humans and AI routinely share tasks or take complementary roles depending on context, fatigue, and the stakes involved. Governing that distribution -- balancing efficiency, oversight, and human capability -- remains an open problem. This paper presents Human-AI Adaptive Symbiosis (HAAS), an implemented framework for adaptive task allocation in software engineering and manufacturing. HAAS combines two coupled components: a rule-based expert system that enforces governance constraints before any learning occurs, and a contextual-bandit learner that selects among feasible collaboration modes from outcome feedback. Task-agent fit is represented through five auditable cognitive dimensions and a five-mode autonomy spectrum -- from human-only to fully autonomous -- embedded in a reproducible benchmark spanning both domains. Three empirical findings emerge. First, governance is not a binary switch but a tunable design variable: tighter constraints predictably convert autonomous AI assignments into supervised collaborations, with domain-specific costs and benefits. Second, in manufacturing, stronger governance can improve operational performance and reduce fatigue simultaneously -- a workload-buffering effect that contradicts the usual framing of governance as pure overhead. Third, no single governance setting dominates across all contexts; moderate governance becomes increasingly competitive as the learner accumulates experience within the governed action space. Together, these findings position HAAS as a pre-deployment workbench for comparing and inspecting human--AI allocation policies before organisational commitment.

</details>


### [186] [Standing on the Shoulders of Giants: Stabilized Knowledge Distillation for Cross--Language Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02860)
*Mohamad Khajezade,Fatemeh H. Fard,Mohamed Sami Shehata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识蒸馏框架，将DeepSeek-R1的推理能力迁移至紧凑型开源模型（Phi3、Qwen-Coder），并结合响应稳定化方法（强制结论提示、二分类头、对比分类头），以提升跨语言代码克隆检测的可靠性和效率。实验表明该方法在多个语言对下提升了模型性能并大幅降低推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型用于跨语言代码克隆检测时，作为黑盒系统存在成本高、不可复现、隐私风险及输出格式不可靠等问题；紧凑型开源模型则难以遵循推理导向的提示且输出无法一致映射为二分类标签。

Method: 构建知识蒸馏框架，将DeepSeek-R1的推理能力迁移至Phi3和Qwen-Coder（使用LoRA微调）；利用Project CodeNet生成跨语言代码对构造推理导向的合成训练数据；提出三种响应稳定化方法：强制结论提示、二分类分类头、对比分类头。

Result: 在Python-Java、Rust-Java、Rust-Python、Rust-Ruby四个语言对上的实验表明，知识蒸馏稳定提升了紧凑型模型的可靠性，在分布偏移场景下尤其显著；分类头变体相比生成式推理大幅降低了推理时间。

Conclusion: 推理导向的知识蒸馏结合响应稳定化方法，使紧凑型开源模型在跨语言代码克隆检测任务中更加实用和可靠。

Abstract: Cross-language code clone detection (X-CCD) is challenging because semantically equivalent programs written in different languages often share little surface similarity. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for semantic clone detection, their use as black-box systems raises concerns about cost, reproducibility, privacy, and unreliable output formatting. In particular, compact open-source models often struggle to follow reasoning-oriented prompts and to produce outputs that can be consistently mapped to binary clone labels.
  To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from DeepSeek-R1 into compact open-source student models for X-CCD. Using cross-language code pairs derived from Project CodeNet, we construct reasoning-oriented synthetic training data and fine-tune Phi3 and Qwen-Coder with LoRA adapters. We further introduce response stabilization methods, including forced conclusion prompting, a binary classification head, and a contrastive classification head, and evaluate model behavior using both predictive metrics and response rate.
  Experiments on Python--Java, Rust--Java, Rust--Python, and Rust--Ruby show that knowledge distillation consistently improves the reliability of compact models and often improves predictive performance, especially under distribution shift. In addition, classification-head variants substantially reduce inference time compared to generation-based inference. Overall, our results show that reasoning-oriented distillation combined with response stabilization makes compact open-source models more practical and reliable for X-CCD detection.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [E-MIA: Exam-Style Black-Box Membership Inference Attacks against RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00955)
*Zelin Guan,Shengda Zhuo,Zeyan Li,Jinchun He,Wangjie Qiu,Zhiming Zheng,Shuqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出E-MIA方法，将目标文档中的可验证硬证据转化为客观可评分的考试题（填空/简答/单选/判断），通过聚合多道证据导向问题的得分作为成员推断信号，在保持查询自然隐蔽的同时提升黑盒RAG成员推断攻击的成员/非成员可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG成员推断攻击（MIA）方法存在软信号（如语义相似度）导致成员/非成员得分分布重叠、阈值不稳定，或显式确认探测意图明显易被拒绝和检测的问题，亟需一种更隐蔽且判别力更强的攻击方法。

Method: 将目标文档中的可验证硬证据（细粒度细节、专有名词/术语、定义性陈述、元数据线索、因果/约束关系）转化为四种客观可评分的问答题型（填空题FB、简答题SC、单选题MC、判断题T/F），聚合多道证据导向问题的得分作为成员推断信号。

Result: 在多个数据集和多样化RAG配置下，E-MIA在严格设置下提升了成员/非成员的可分离性，同时保持了自然、隐蔽的查询行为，并分析了问题组合和考试长度对攻击效果的影响。

Conclusion: E-MIA通过将硬证据转化为客观考试的方式，有效解决了现有RAG-MIA方法中软信号不稳定的问题，实现了更隐蔽且判别力更强的文档级成员推断攻击。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) equips large language models (LLMs) with external evidence by retrieving documents at inference time, but it also turns the retrieval corpusinto a sensitive asset. Under a black-box setting, an adversary given a candidate document can infer whether it has been ingested into the RAG knowledge base (i.e., document-level membership inference) solely from query response interactions, thereby leaking corpus coverage and the existence of sensitive topics. Existing RAG MIA methods either rely on soft signals such as semantic similarity, which often yield overlapping member/non-member score distributions and unstable thresholds, or employ explicit confirmation probes whose intent is conspicuous and thus prone to refusal and detection. We propose E-MIA, which converts verifiable hard evidence in the target document (e.g., fine-grained details, proper nouns/technical terms, definitional statements, metadata cues, and causal/constraint relations) into an exam with four objectively gradable question types (FB/SC/MC/T/F), and uses the aggregated exam score across multiple evidence targeted questions as the membership signal. Experiments across multiple datasets and diverse RAG configurations demonstrate that E-MIA improves member/non-member separability in stringent settings while preserving natural, stealthy queries, and we further analyze the impact of question composition and exam length on attack effectiveness.

</details>


### [188] [SRTJ: Self-Evolving Rule-Driven Training-Free LLM Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00974)
*Jindong Li,Ying Liu,Yali Fu,Jinjing Zhu,Leyao Wang,Menglin Yang,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需模型参数更新的自进化规则驱动型越狱框架（SRTJ），通过经验驱动的攻击生成与基于回答集编程（ASP）的规则选择及约束感知组合，利用迭代验证器反馈协同优化成功策略与失败模式分析，构建层次化多级规则记忆（长/中/短期），在HarmBench基准上实现了对多种目标LLM的强健且稳定的越狱攻击性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动越狱方法缺乏对成功和失败攻击经验的系统性利用，缺乏在不同约束下组合和选择可重用攻击规则的原则性机制，导致无法积累可迁移知识并可靠地适应不同目标和不断演化的安全机制。

Method: SRTJ框架由三部分组成：(1) 经验驱动的攻击生成，通过交互反馈生成攻击策略；(2) 基于回答集编程（ASP）的规则选择与约束感知组合，实现原则性的规则选取；(3) 层次化多级规则记忆，将蒸馏的攻击知识显式组织为长期（稳定可迁移策略）、中期和短期（瞬态自适应行为）规则，平衡探索与利用。整个过程无需更新模型参数。

Result: 在HarmBench基准上的大量实验表明，SRTJ在不同目标LLM上实现了强健且稳定的攻击性能，相比于现有越狱方法展现出更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SRTJ通过规则驱动的自进化机制，无需参数更新即可有效积累和迁移攻击知识，为越狱攻击提供了一种更系统化、可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly equipped with safety alignment mechanisms, yet recent studies demonstrate that they remain vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks that elicit harmful behaviors without explicit policy violations. While a growing body of work has explored automated jailbreak strategies, existing methods face several fundamental challenges, including the lack of systematic utilization of both successful and failed attack experiences, as well as the absence of principled mechanisms for composing and selecting reusable attack rules under diverse constraints. As a result, existing methods struggle to accumulate transferable knowledge over time and to reliably adapt attack strategies across different targets and evolving safety mechanisms. To address these issues, we propose a Self-Evolving Rule-Driven Training-Free Jailbreak (SRTJ) framework that systematically discovers, composes, and refines attack strategies through interaction and feedback, without updating model parameters. Specifically, SRTJ couples experience-driven attack generation with answer set programming (ASP)-based rule selection and constraint-aware composition, where iterative verifier feedback is leveraged to jointly refine successful strategies and analyze failure patterns. The resulting rule memory evolves in a hierarchical multi-level manner, explicitly organizing distilled attack knowledge into long-term, middle-term, and short-term rules, thereby capturing both stable transferable strategies and transient adaptive behaviors to effectively balance exploration and exploitation across attack attempts. Extensive experiments on mainstream jailbreak benchmark (HarmBench) demonstrate that SRTJ achieves strong and stable attack performance across different target LLMs, while exhibiting improved robustness and generalization compared to existing jailbreak methods. The code is available at https://github.com/TheSolkatt/SRTJ.

</details>


### [189] [Certified Purity for Cognitive Workflow Executors: From Static Analysis to Cryptographic Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01037)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种认证纯度架构，通过WebAssembly编译限制、密码学签名证书、运行时验证和远程认证四重机制，将认知工作流系统中的治理执行从运行时约定转化为结构性能力边界，消除BEAM虚拟机上五种对抗性绕过类别，实现微秒级验证延迟和低于0.4%的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的三层治理架构依赖模块导入图分析来强制执行纯模块约束（步骤执行者不能产生副作用），但这种机制在BEAM虚拟机上易受对抗性绕过攻击，无法提供结构性安全保障。

Method: 提出四种机制：(1)限制性WebAssembly编译目标，结构上消除效应指令；(2)纯度证书，对执行者二进制文件与导入分类进行密码学签名；(3)运行时验证门，在进入治理管道前拒绝未认证执行者；(4)通过远程认证实现跨组织的可移植治理凭证。

Result: 在四个执行者上评估：验证延迟39-42微秒，完整计划周期低于400微秒，运行时开销低于100ms HTTP请求的0.4%，零确定性发散。

Conclusion: 证明了四个定理：结构性纯度构建、五种BEAM绕过类别的消除、证书完整性、验证门完备性。保证基于显式可信计算基。

Abstract: We present a certified purity architecture that converts governance enforcement in cognitive workflow systems from a runtime convention into a structural capability boundary. A prior three-layer governance architecture proves governance completeness, provenance completeness, and the impossibility of ungoverned effects, conditional on the pure module constraint: that step executors cannot perform effects. That constraint was enforced by module import graph analysis, which is insufficient against adversarial bypass on the BEAM virtual machine. This paper closes the gap through four mechanisms: (1) a restricted WebAssembly compilation target where effect-producing instructions are structurally absent; (2) purity certificates, cryptographically signed proofs binding executor binaries to their import classifications; (3) a runtime verification gate that rejects uncertified executors before they enter the governance pipeline; and (4) portable governance credentials via remote attestation for cross-organizational verification. We prove four theorems: structural purity by construction, bypass elimination for all five BEAM bypass classes, certificate integrity, and gate completeness. The guarantee holds relative to an explicit Trusted Computing Base. Evaluation on four implemented executors shows verification latency of 39--42 us, full plan cycle under 400 us, runtime overhead under 0.4% of a 100 ms HTTP request, and zero determinism divergences across repeated invocations.

</details>


### [190] [A Sentence Relation-Based Approach to Sanitizing Malicious Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01078)
*Soumil Datta,Melissa Umble,Daniel S. Brown,Guanhong Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SONAR 提出基于自然语言推理的提示清理框架，通过构建句子级关系图并使用蕴含/矛盾分数进行连通性剪枝，有效移除注入内容，将攻击成功率降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（LLM检测器、训练方法）难以抵御基于优化的攻击，且泛化能力不足，亟需更鲁棒的提示清理方案。

Method: 1）在用户查询与外部数据间构建句子级关系图；2）以蕴含和矛盾分数作为边权重；3）识别偏离核心任务的句子；4）基于连通性剪枝移除注入种子及其关联邻居，保留良性上下文。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上，SONAR 将攻击成功率降至接近零，显著优于9种已有基线防御方法。

Conclusion: 基于自然语言推理的图剪枝策略能有效防御提示注入攻击，且具有较好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation and tool-integrated LLM agents increasingly depend on external textual sources. This reliance broadens the available attack surface, allowing adversaries to insert malicious instructions that trigger unintended model behaviors. Current defensive measures often utilize LLM-based detectors to filter such content, but these approaches remain vulnerable to optimization-based attacks. Additionally, training-based methods frequently fail to generalize to novel data distributions. To resolve these issues, we introduce SONAR, a prompt sanitization framework that identifies and removes injected content using metrics from natural language inference. Specifically, SONAR constructs a sentence-level relational graph across the user query and external data. By using entailment and contradiction scores as edge weights, the system identifies sentences that deviate from the core task. It then employs connectivity-driven pruning to eliminate flagged injection seeds and their related neighbors while maintaining benign context. Rigorous evaluations across several models and datasets show that SONAR reduces the attack success rate to nearly zero, significantly outperforming nine established baseline defenses.

</details>


### [191] [Revisiting Privacy Leakage in Machine Unlearning: Membership Inference Beyond the Forgotten Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01129)
*Jie Fu,Nima Naderloui,Da Zhong,Yuan Hong,Wendy Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个三分类遗忘成员推断攻击TC-UMIA，证明机器遗忘过程会意外泄露保留集的隐私，并评估了三种防御机制，发现dropout在隐私保护与模型精度间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要关注被遗忘数据的隐私风险，忽视了遗忘过程中保留集（未删除数据）可能产生的隐私泄露问题。

Method: 提出TC-UMIA（三分类遗忘成员推断攻击），是一种基于模型遗忘前后预测差异的群体级推断框架，能够区分遗忘集、保留集和未见集三类数据。

Result: 在五种最先进的遗忘算法和六个真实数据集上验证了：(i) 遗忘操作会增加保留集的成员推断风险；(ii) TC-UMIA在多种模型架构、数据集和遗忘方法上均有效；(iii) 评估了三种防御机制（仅标签输出、dropout、差分隐私），发现dropout在隐私保护与模型精度间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘中的隐私风险并非仅限于被遗忘数据，保留集同样面临威胁；隐私保护与模型精度之间存在根本性的权衡关系。

Abstract: Machine unlearning (MU) has emerged as a key mechanism for ensuring data privacy and regulatory compliance by enabling models to forget specific training samples. However, recent studies have shown that the removal of data can inadvertently introduce privacy leakages to the retain set,i.e., data that remain in the model after unlearning. In this paper, we extend the scope of privacy analysis in unlearning to the often-overlooked retained data. We introduce TC-UMIA, the first tri-class unlearning membership inference attack. TC-UMIA is a population-level inference framework that leverages model predictions before and after unlearning to distinguish among the forget, retain, and unseen set. Extensive experiments on five state-of-the-art unlearning algorithms and six real-world datasets demonstrate that: (i) unlearning can introduce additional privacy risks to the retain set, making it more susceptible to membership inference attacks; (ii) TC-UMIA is effective across a wide range of model architectures, datasets, and MU approaches. Beyond launching the attack, we rigorously evaluate three defense mechanisms, namely label-only outputs, dropout, and differential privacy, to mitigate the privacy risks posed by TC- UMIA. Our results reveal a fundamental trade-off between privacy protection and model accuracy, with the dropout approach offering the most favorable balance.

</details>


### [192] [When Embedding-Based Defenses Fail: Rethinking Safety in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01133)
*Lingxi Zhang,Guangtao Zheng,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于嵌入的防御在多智能体系统（MAS）中的根本局限性：攻击者可通过使恶意消息的文本嵌入接近良性消息来绕过检测。作者提出利用置信度分数（logits）在通信过程中剪枝或降权消息，实验表明该方法能提升鲁棒性，且置信度信号的有效性随通信轮次衰减，早期干预至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的多智能体系统在复杂任务中表现优异，但智能体间的通信为恶意智能体传播虚假信息、操控群体决策创造了攻击面。现有基于嵌入的防御依赖于恶意与良性消息文本嵌入的清晰分离，但攻击者可构造嵌入接近良性消息的恶意文本，从而绕过检测。作者旨在分析这一失败模式并探索更鲁棒的防御方法。

Method: 首先从理论上分析了基于嵌入的防御的局限性，并通过三种攻击（Slow Drift、Benign Wrapper、Chaos Seeding）进行实证验证。进一步提出利用置信度分数（logits）在MAS通信过程中对消息进行剪枝或降权，以此作为替代或补充防御策略。实验在多种模型、数据集和通信拓扑上评估了该方法的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，基于置信度的防御方法在多种设置下均提升了鲁棒性。同时发现，置信度信号的有效性随通信轮次增加而衰减，即早期干预的防御效果更好。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入的MAS防御存在根本性局限，而置信度分数提供了一种有效的补充防御途径。置信度信号的时效性衰减特征强调了早期干预的重要性，为未来MAS攻击与防御研究提供了启示。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered multi-agent systems (MAS) enable agents to communicate and share information, achieving strong performance on complex tasks. However, this communication also creates an attack surface where malicious agents can propagate misinformation and manipulate group decisions, undermining MAS safety. Existing embedding-based defenses aim to detect and prune suspicious agents, but their effectiveness depends on a clear separation between the text embeddings of malicious and benign messages. Attackers can circumvent such defenses by crafting messages whose embeddings lie close to benign ones. We analyze this failure mode theoretically and validate it empirically with three attacks, Slow Drift, Benign Wrapper, and Chaos Seeding. Our analysis further reveals a fundamental limitation of embedding-based defenses: because they rely solely on the text embeddings, they ignore token-level confidence signals such as logits, which can remain informative when embeddings are not distinguishable under attack. We propose using confidence scores to prune or down-weight messages during MAS communication. Experiments show improved robustness across models, datasets, and communication topologies. Moreover, we find that the effectiveness of confidence signals decays over communication rounds, highlighting the importance of early intervention. This insights can inform and inspire future work on MAS attacks and defenses.

</details>


### [193] [Phishing Detection in Ethereum via Temporal Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01207)
*Cong Wu,Jing Chen,Siqi Lin,Hongda Li,Ziming Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PhishEye，一种全动态自监督系统，将以太坊交易建模为异质时序属性多图，使用时序图对比学习检测钓鱼攻击。在161,658个地址和416,541笔交易的数据集上，钓鱼交易检测F1达87.23%、AUC达98.43%；钓鱼账户检测F1达94.19%、AUC达98.03%。实际部署中提前识别1,803个未知钓鱼地址，避免超20亿美元损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼检测方法存在三大局限：(1)静态图学习无法捕捉钓鱼模式的时序演化；(2)半动态方法（静态GNN+LSTM）难以应对区块链交易的非规则性和突发性；(3)忽视以太坊交易的异质性，将异构图视为同质图处理，且依赖大量标注数据的监督学习，难以适应新型钓鱼威胁。

Method: PhishEye将以太坊交易建模为异质时序属性多图（heterogeneous temporal attributed multi-graph），并提出一种新颖的时序图对比学习模型（temporal graph contrastive learning），同时捕获时序模式和异构交易类型，采用全动态自监督范式进行训练。

Result: 钓鱼交易检测：F1=87.23%, AUC=98.43%；钓鱼账户检测：F1=94.19%, AUC=98.03%。实际部署（2023/5/1-2024/7/31）识别1,803个未知钓鱼地址，避免超20亿美元损失。

Conclusion: PhishEye通过全动态、自监督、异质图建模的方式，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，显著提升了加密货币钓鱼攻击检测性能，并能在实际部署中提前预警，带来巨大的经济防护效益。

Abstract: Blockchain and decentralized finance have revolutionized the financial ecosystem while simultaneously exposing it to cryptocurrency phishing attacks. Existing phishing detection methods primarily rely on graph learning, but they face significant limitations. Static graph learning approaches fail to account for the temporal evolution of phishing patterns, while semi-dynamic methods, such as those combining static GNNs with LSTM, struggle to capture the irregular and bursty nature of blockchain transactions. Moreover, these methods overlook the diversity of Ethereum transactions, treating them as homogeneous graphs, and heavily rely on supervised learning, which requires extensive labeled data that is not readily available. These limitations reduce their adaptability to emerging phishing threats.
  In this paper, we present PhishEye, a fully dynamic self-supervised system that monitors on-chain transactions to detect phishing activities. PhishEye formulates Ethereum transactions as a heterogeneous temporal attributed multi-graph and incorporates a novel temporal graph contrastive learning model, which captures both temporal patterns and heterogeneous transaction types. The evaluation on a dataset of 161,658 addresses and 416,541 transactions shows that PhishEye outperforms existing methods, achieving an F1 score of 87.23% and an AUC of 98.43% for phishing transaction detection, and an F1 score of 94.19% and an AUC of 98.03% for phishing account detection. In real-world deployment from May 1, 2023 to July 31, 2024, PhishEye identified 1,803 previously unknown phishing addresses, providing early alerts that helped prevent losses exceeding 2 billion USD.

</details>


### [194] [Write-Domain Separation and Non-Custodial Enforcement: A Structural Impossibility in Account-Based Ledgers, with a Commitment-Based Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01210)
*Matthias Hauser*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于账户的账本（标准EOA、ERC-4337智能账户、EIP-7702委托EOA）中，协议能否在不托管资产且无需所有者配合的情况下强制执行资产的未来处置。形式化定义了非托管强制产权负担（NCEE）规范，证明了满足密钥主权公理的账本无法实现NCEE，提出了资产-授权耦合公理（AAC）作为必要前提，并引入了一种称为envelope的原语在承诺式私有状态账本上实现NCEE，提供了Noir/OuraHonk参考实现及经济分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有账户模型将控制密钥持有者置于资产授权的顶点，限制了协议对资产未来处置的强制执行能力。作者希望在不采取托管且无需所有者配合的前提下，实现协议层面的资产约束，填补现有区块链访问控制模型的结构性空白。

Method: 首先形式化定义NCEE（四个性质：自助托管、转移限制、不可撤销、无需许可执行）。通过密钥主权公理（KS）证明标准账户模型无法实现NCEE。定义资产-授权耦合（AAC）作为必要条件。提出envelope原语——在承诺式私有状态账本上绑定note、条件树和重分配意图到协议维护的标记集合，通过note随机性而非所有者密钥导出新的产权负担命名空间空化器。给出三个部署模板并构建安全游戏验证。

Result: 证明了满足KS的账本无法实现NCEE；标准EOA、ERC-4337和EIP-7702均满足KS。证明了AAC是转移二分资产设置下实现NCEE的必要条件。Envelope原语在所述密码学假设和标记集合注册表不可变部署假设下实现了NCEE。Noir/OuraHonk参考实现提供了气体消耗、递归聚合基准和经济可行性分析。

Conclusion: 传统基于密钥的账户模型在结构上限制了非托管资产约束的实现，而envelope原语通过分离授权机制与密钥主权，为需要条件性资产约束的协议（如借贷、托管、DeFi等）提供了可行的解决方案。该工作为区块链访问控制模型开辟了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Account-based ledgers -- standard externally-owned accounts (EOAs), ERC-4337 smart accounts, post-Pectra EIP-7702 delegated EOAs -- place the holder of the controlling key at the apex of asset authorization. We ask a structural question about ledger access control: under this authorization model, can a protocol enforce the future disposition of an asset without taking custody and without requiring the owner's cooperation at enforcement time? We formalize the target as Non-Custodial Enforced Encumbrance (NCEE), a four-property specification covering self-custody, transition restriction, irrevocability, and permissionless enforcement.
  We define the Key Sovereignty Axiom (KS) and prove that any ledger satisfying KS cannot realize NCEE; standard EOAs, ERC-4337 smart accounts, and EIP-7702 delegated EOAs satisfy KS for their standard asset paths. We define Asset-Authorization Coupling (AAC) and prove it necessary for NCEE in the transfer-dichotomous asset setting. To witness the positive side, we introduce the envelope, a primitive for commitment-based private-state ledgers that binds a note, a condition tree, and a redistribution intent to protocol-maintained marker sets, separating ordinary spend nullifiers from a new encumbrance-namespace nullifier derived from note randomness rather than the owner key. We prove the envelope realizes NCEE under stated cryptographic assumptions and a deployment assumption that the marker-set registry is immutable; three concrete deployment templates are given.
  We define games for encumbrance integrity, settlement security, key-compromise resilience, and encumbrance indistinguishability. A reference implementation in Noir and UltraHonk supports the empirical claims, with gas measurements, recursive aggregation benchmarks, and a practical-economics analysis.

</details>


### [195] [From Stealthy Data Fabrication to Unsafe Driving: Realistic Scenario Attacks on Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01301)
*Qingzhao Zhang,Runting Zhang,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对协同感知系统的隐蔽性数据伪造攻击，通过微小姿态扰动绕过检测阈值，经下游模块传播引发不安全驾驶行为，并在多场景下达到90%以上攻击成功率，同时提出一种基于局部安全关键区域异常检测的防御方法，检测率达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对协同感知的攻击研究多基于人工构造场景，缺乏对动态真实驾驶环境中攻击可行性的评估；既有攻击易被检测，其能否在端到端系统中诱发安全关键后果尚不明确。

Method: 提出一种隐蔽的、场景真实的数据伪造攻击，通过对共享感知结果中已有物体的位姿进行微小扰动（低于检测阈值），使误差经目标跟踪和轨迹预测等下游模块传播放大，最终导致不安全驾驶决策；并设计在线场景感知攻击框架，动态优化攻击策略以适应实时交通条件。

Result: 在OPV2V和V2X-Real数据集上，攻击在诱导检测错误方面成功率超90%，在最多50%的场景中触发安全关键行为（如不必要的急刹车），并能有效规避现有最优防御方法；提出的局部安全关键区域异常检测方法达到80%的检测率，而现有最优方法仅11%。

Conclusion: 协同感知系统面临来自隐蔽性数据伪造攻击的严重安全威胁，现有防御手段不足以应对此类微小扰动攻击；聚焦局部安全关键区域的异常检测是一种有效的缓解方向。

Abstract: Collaborative perception allows connected and autonomous vehicles (CAVs) to improve perception by sharing sensory data, but it also introduces security risks from manipulated inputs. Prior work shows that attackers can spoof or remove objects by fabricating shared data, yet the practicality of such attacks in real-world driving remains unclear. Existing attacks are often detectable or evaluated in manually constructed scenarios, leaving open whether they can induce safety-critical outcomes in dynamic environments. To bridge this gap, we present a stealthy, scenario-realistic data fabrication attack that induces unsafe driving behaviors through end-to-end system effects. Instead of creating large, easily detectable anomalies, our attack subtly manipulates the poses of existing objects in shared perception results, keeping perturbations below detection thresholds. These small errors are then propagated through downstream modules, including object tracking and trajectory prediction, leading to significant deviations in predicted behaviors and ultimately unsafe driving decisions. We further design an online, scenario-aware attack framework that adapts to dynamic traffic conditions and optimizes attack strategies at runtime. Experiments on OPV2V and V2X-Real demonstrate that the attack achieves over 90% success in inducing detection errors and triggers safety-critical behaviors, such as unnecessary hard braking, in up to 50% of scenarios, while largely evading state-of-the-art defenses. We also propose a mitigation that focuses on detecting anomalies in localized, safety-critical regions, achieving an 80% detection rate on the small pose perturbation compared to 11% for the best existing methods.

</details>


### [196] [LocalAlign: Enabling Generalizable Prompt Injection Defense via Generation of Near-Target Adversarial Examples for Alignment Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01462)
*Yuyang Gong,Zihao Wang,Jiawei Liu,XiaoFeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LocalAlign，一种基于对抗训练的提示注入防御方法。该方法通过自动生成"近但错误"的对抗样本（使模型对注入命令的响应接近正确响应但仍错误），并结合边距感知对齐算法，在正确响应周围构建更紧致的鲁棒边界，从而实现对提示注入攻击的更好泛化防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入防御主要通过微调让模型区分可信命令与不可信数据，但仅针对固定手工攻击目标训练，导致在正确响应周围形成松散边界，易被绕过；尤其在注入命令诱导的响应接近正确响应的真实场景中泛化能力差。

Method: LocalAlign包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用提示和单次推理步骤自动生成"近但错误"的对抗样本，在数据部分嵌入命令使模型输出接近正确但实际错误的响应；(2) 边距感知对齐算法，量化每个样本到正确响应的距离，为更接近的样本赋予更大训练权重，以应对对抗样本质量差异大的问题。

Result: 通过生成近但错误的对抗样本，在正确响应周围建立更紧致的鲁棒边界，即使微小响应偏移也会受到显式惩罚，从而提升防御的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LocalAlign通过对抗训练范式为提示注入防御提供了更通用的解决方案，有效弥补了现有方法在真实场景下边界松散、泛化不足的缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly embedded into systems that interact with user data, retrieved web content, and external tools, creating a new attack surface: prompt injection, where malicious commands embedded in untrusted data override the trusted command and induce unintended behavior. Existing defenses mainly rely on fine-tuning the model to preserve an explicit boundary between trusted commands and the untrusted data portion, so that the model learns to prioritize the trusted field and ignore malicious commands in data. However, we observe that while these defenses can block obviously malicious responses caused by injected commands, they generalize poorly to real-world scenarios where the model's response to the injected command is much nearer to the correct response. This is because existing methods typically train against only a fixed set of hand-crafted attack targets, which yields a loose boundary around the correct response and leaves it easier to bypass.
  To address this challenge, we propose LocalAlign, a more generalizable prompt injection defense inspired by adversarial training. LocalAlign automatically and efficiently generates adversarial examples in which the command embedded in the data portion induces a response that stays near to the correct response while still being wrong. We generate such near-but-wrong adversarial examples using prompting and a single inference step. This design enforces a tighter robustness boundary around the correct response: even small response shifts induced by commands in untrusted data are explicitly penalized. Moreover, the resulting adversarial examples can vary substantially in quality across samples. To address this issue, we further introduce a margin-aware alignment algorithm that quantifies each sample's distance to the correct response and assigns larger training weight to nearer ones.

</details>


### [197] [Toward a Principled Framework for Agent Safety Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01644)
*Shuyi Lin,Anshuman Suri,Alina Oprea,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出BOA框架，通过搜索而非采样来评估LLM智能体安全性，能在给定似然预算下发现贪心或少量采样评估遗漏的不安全轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全评估使用贪心或少量采样展开，只能报告单一的安全/不安全比率，忽略了低概率但不可忽略的行为可能导致的长尾不安全轨迹。

Method: BOA框架在给定部署配置（模型、解码器、提示词、环境、评判器、似然预算）下，搜索预算内的轨迹空间并报告安全分数。搜索同时覆盖单轮LLM内部和智能体-环境交互树，通过批处理解码/评判、前缀缓存和分块树扩展实现实用化。

Result: 在智能体安全工作负载上，BOA发现了贪心和采样评估遗漏的不安全轨迹；可用于在同一尺度上对模型、防御和攻击进行排序，GPU成本可控。

Conclusion: 智能体安全应通过搜索而非采样来衡量，BOA提供了有效的安全评估和排名工具。

Abstract: LLM agents emit actions, not just text, and once taken, those actions often cannot be undone. Yet today's agent-safety evaluations run greedy or a few sampled rollouts and report a single safe/unsafe rate -- blind to the long-tail trajectories where unsafe behavior may arise from low-probability but non-negligible actions.
  We argue agent safety should be measured by search, not sampling. We apply BOA, a framework that, given a deployment configuration (model, decoder, prompt, environment, judger, likelihood budget), searches the in-budget trajectory space and reports a safety score: the probability the agent stays safe under the configuration. BOA searches both within a single LLM round and across the agent-environment interaction tree under a given likelihood budget, and makes search practical via batched decoding/judging, prefix caching, and chunked tree expansion. On agent-safety workloads, BOA discovers unsafe trajectories that greedy and sampled evaluations miss. BOA can additionally be used for ranking models, defenses, and attacks, all on the same scale, with manageable GPU costs.

</details>


### [198] [Limit Properties at Critical Indices of Linear Canonical Riesz Potentials and Their Applications to Security of Multi-Image Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01654)
*Zunwei Fu,Dachun Yang,Shuhui Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文引入线性正则Riesz势(LCRP)，给出其符号表示，研究不同函数下LCRP的收敛/发散性，并基于此提出非对称级联LCRP多图像加密方法，构建高效安全的密码系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Riesz势对某些函数（如光栅函数）发散，而LCRP通过啁啾函数的作用使其收敛；凸多边形特征函数的Riesz势在边界处极限与函数值不同。这些收敛/发散特性可被用于图像加密与解密的安全性设计。

Method: 1) 定义线性正则Riesz势(LCRP)并推导其在线性正则变换下的符号表示；2) 分析光栅函数和凸多边形特征函数的LCRP收敛/发散性；3) 基于LCRP及其逆算子（线性正则拉普拉斯算子）设计非对称级联LCRP多图像加密方法。

Result: 光栅函数的经典Riesz势发散，但LCRP收敛；凸多边形特征函数的Riesz势在非边界点收敛到自身，边界处不同；基于LCRP的加密方法在敏感性、统计、噪声攻击和遮挡攻击分析中表现出鲁棒性和安全性，单图像加密效率优于基于分数阶Riesz势的方法。

Conclusion: LCRP在临界指标下对"好"的Schwartz函数收敛、对"坏"的离散图像函数发散的差异，本质影响了图像加密与解密的安全性，为设计新型密码系统提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this article we introduce the linear canonical Riesz potential (for short, LCRP) and give its symbol in terms of linear canonical transforms. Driven by image processing, we establish the convergence/divergence of these LCRPs for different kinds of functions. Concretely, for grating functions, we prove that their classical Riesz potentials diverge, whereas their LCRP converge due to the key role of chirp functions. For the characteristic function ${\mathbf 1}_P$ of a convex polygon $P$, we show that the limit of its Riesz potential at any non-boundary point $\boldsymbol{x}$ equals ${\mathbf 1}_P(\boldsymbol{x})$, but its limit at the boundaries differ from ${\mathbf 1}_P$, while it is known that, for any Schwartz function $f$, the limit of its Riesz potential at any point $\boldsymbol{x}$ always equals $f(\boldsymbol{x})$. Based on these and the inverse operator of the LCRP (namely the linear canonical Laplacian operator), we propose an asymmetric cascaded LCRP method for the multi-image encryption and create an efficient and secure cryptosystem. Systematic security evaluations, including sensitivity, statistical, noise attack, and occlusion attack analyses, demonstrate its robustness and its security. Even for a single image, the proposed method is more efficient than the known encryption approach based on the fractional Riesz potential. The novelty of these results lies in that the convergence and the divergence of LCRTs at the critical indices, respectively, for ``good" Schwartz functions and for ``bad" discrete image functions essentially affect the security of image encryption and decryption.

</details>


### [199] [Class-Aware Adaptive Differential Privacy in Deep Learning for Sensor-Based Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01679)
*Joydeb Kumar Sana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种类别感知自适应差分隐私（CA-ADP）框架，结合3D CNN-BiLSTM混合架构，通过根据小批量中类别组成动态调整梯度噪声，在保护隐私的同时提升跌倒检测性能，在三个公开数据集上F-score分别提升3.3%、8.5%和7.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器活动数据在跌倒检测中非常有效，但这类数据高度敏感，存在严重隐私问题。现有隐私方法对所有训练样本施加统一噪声，影响了预测性能。

Method: 提出CA-ADP框架，集成混合3D CNN-BiLSTM架构。CA-ADP根据每个小批量的类别组成动态调整添加到梯度上的噪声幅度，在保证隐私的同时减轻性能退化。论文还提供了(ε,δ)-差分隐私的形式化保证和隐私-效用权衡分析。

Result: 在SisFall、UP-Fall和MobiAct三个数据集上，CA-ADP模型相比传统隐私模型F-score分别提升3.3%、8.5%和7.5%；Wilcoxon符号秩检验证实该机制一致优于传统差分隐私方法。

Conclusion: CA-ADP框架是一种有效的隐私保护跌倒检测方法，在真实医疗场景中既提供了形式化隐私保证，又实现了竞争性能，解决了现有研究中隐私保证被忽视的问题。

Abstract: Fall detection is a critical task in healthcare, particularly for elderly people. Timely fall detection and treatment can prevent severe injuries. Sensor-based activity data can be used to detect fall. However, this data are highly sensitive and raises significant privacy concerns. Existing privacy approaches apply uniform noise across all training samples, which affects the prediction performance. To address this limitation, we propose a Class-Aware Adaptive Differential Privacy (CA-ADP) framework integrated with a hybrid 3D Convolutional Neural Network and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (3D CNN-BiLSTM) architecture. The CA-ADP mechanism dynamically adjusts the magnitude of noise added to gradients based on the class composition of each mini-batch. This process ensures privacy while mitigates performance degradation. We formally analyze the $(ε,δ)$-Differential Privacy guarantee and provide a privacy-utility trade-off analysis. The proposed method is evaluated on three public benchmark datasets, namely SisFall, UP-Fall, and MobiAct. The experimental results show that the proposed privacy model achieves improvements of 3.3\%, 8.5\%, and 7.5\% over the conventional privacy-based model in terms of F-score for the SisFall, UP-Fall, and MobiAct datasets, respectively. Comparisons with prior studies show that the CA-AD based framework achieves competitive performance and provides formal privacy guarantees, which are largely overlooked in existing studies. Wilcoxon signed-rank tests confirm that the proposed mechanism consistently outperforms conventional differential privacy. Those results establish the proposed CA-ADP framework as an effective approach to privacy-preserving fall detection in real-world healthcare settings.

</details>


### [200] [Automated Channel Fault Analysis with Tofu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01721)
*Jacob Ginesin,Max von Hippel,Cristina Nita-Rotaru*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分布式协议信道故障的自动化分析方法，并实现了工具Tofu，能够合成攻击轨迹或证明协议在LTL规范下无此类攻击，并在TCP上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式协议研究通常假设信道存在故障或恶意攻击，但形式化验证工作各自定义信道模型，缺乏统一、自动化的信道故障分析方法。

Method: 提出自动化信道故障分析的方法论，实现工具Tofu，通过对任意线性时序逻辑（LTL）协议规范进行穷举状态空间搜索，合成基于信道故障的攻击轨迹或证明其不存在。

Result: Tofu提供了完备且正确的分析能力，并在TCP协议上成功应用，展示了其实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布式协议的信道故障分析提供了系统化、自动化的解决方案，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Distributed protocols are the linchpin of the modern internet, underpinning every internet service. This has in turn motivated a massive body of research ensuring the security, reliability, and performance of distributed protocols. In these works, a wide-ranging assumption is that distributed protocols operate over faulty or attacker-controlled channels, where messages can be arbitrarily inserted, dropped, replayed, or reordered. Formal verification work targeting distributed protocols typically defines its own notion of faulty or malicious channels, then constructively proves their protocol is correct with respect to it. In this work we take a fundamentally different approach: we develop a rigorous methodology for automatically conducting channel fault analysis on distributed protocols, and we introduce Tofu, a generalizable tool that implements our methodology. Tofu provides sound, complete analysis, synthesizing channel fault-based attack traces on arbitrary linear temporal logic (LTL) protocol specifications or proving the absence of such through an exhaustive state-space search. We demonstrate the applicability of Tofu by employing it to study TCP.

</details>


### [201] [AgenticVM: Agentic AI for Adaptive Software Vulnerability Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01739)
*Asrul Arifin,Hussain Ahmad,Yiyao Zhang,Diksha Goel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticVM，一个集成大语言模型与安全工具的多智能体框架，用于自动化漏洞检测、评估、优先级排序和报告。该框架在多个评估场景中实现高达98%的告警缩减（如从3,983条发现缩减至82条高优先级项），并以89.3%的准确率预测缺失的CVSS属性，显著提升优先级排序效率并降低分析师工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统规模和复杂度不断增长，漏洞管理面临告警量大、工具链碎片化和人工分诊流程繁重等挑战，亟需自动化解决方案来提升效率。

Method: AgenticVM是一个多智能体框架，结合了基于规则的处理、基于BERT的CVSS预测模块以及专门的LLM驱动智能体，利用来自国家漏洞数据库（NVD）和欧盟漏洞数据库等多源数据进行漏洞管理自动化。

Result: 在多个评估场景中，AgenticVM实现了高达98%的告警缩减（从3,983条发现压缩至82条高优先级项），并以89.3%的准确率预测缺失的CVSS属性。

Conclusion: AgenticVM证明了多智能体LLM框架在提升漏洞管理优先级排序效率和降低分析师工作负载方面的有效性，同时不牺牲风险可见性，并为实际部署中的智能体分解、工具-LLM集成和人机协作治理提供了设计洞见。

Abstract: As software systems grow in scale and complexity, vulnerability management is increasingly strained by high alert volumes, fragmented toolchains, and manual triage processes. We introduce AgenticVM, a multi-agent framework that integrates large language models with security tools to automate vulnerability detection, assessment, prioritization, and reporting. AgenticVM combines rule-based processing, a BERT-based CVSS prediction module, and specialised LLM-driven agents, leveraging data from sources such as the National Vulnerability Database and the European Union Vulnerability Database. Across multiple evaluation scenarios, AgenticVM reduces raw scanner outputs into compact, actionable queues, achieving up to 98% alert reduction (e.g., from 3,983 findings to 82 high-priority items), while predicting missing CVSS attributes with 89.3% accuracy. These results demonstrate improved prioritisation efficiency and reduced analyst workload without compromising risk visibility. Beyond performance, the framework provides practical design insights into agent decomposition, tool-LLM integration, and human-in-the-loop governance for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [202] [Architectural Obsolescence of Unhardened Agentic-AI Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01740)
*Alfredo Metere*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现开源单用户Agentic-AI网关OpenClaw在检测四种关键动作偏差（F1-F4）时完全失效（召回率0.000），而通过引入七个特定运行时结构（如双条件检查器、哈希链审计日志等）的enclawed-oss实现了完美检测（P=R=F1=准确率=1.000），证明安全差距源于架构而非参数配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic-AI运行时需要捕获工具调用、消息发送和设备控制中的四种偏差（F1-F4），这是关键安全属性。然而现有最成熟的单用户网关OpenClaw在这方面的能力未知，本文旨在评估并改进。

Method: 设计了1600样本模板基线测试和10种LLM跨模型泛化测试，对比OpenClaw与enclawed-oss在检测F1-F4四种偏差上的表现。enclawed-oss通过引入七个运行时结构实现安全加固，包括双条件检查器、哈希链审计日志、扩展准入网关、双层出口守卫、Bell-LaPadula分类策略、模块签名信任根和引导密封。

Result: OpenClaw在所有混淆矩阵单元上召回率为0.000，完全无法检测任何偏差。enclawed-oss达到P=R=F1=准确率=1.000。对DLP正则表达式目录做六行追加扩展，enclawed-oss的每通道F3检测率提升14.6%且精度不变，而OpenClaw因架构限制无法应用同样的修改。

Conclusion: 未加固的Agentic-AI运行时在架构上已过时，存在严格更优的替代方案并可立即采用，差距需要通过重新架构而非配置来解决。

Abstract: An agentic-AI runtime issues tool calls, sends messages, and actuates devices on behalf of an LLM. Catching the four ways an action can diverge from its audit record -- F1 gate-bypass, F2 audit-forgery, silent host failure, F4 wrong-target, -- is a load-bearing safety property of any such runtime. We show that upstream OpenClaw, the most engineered single-user agentic-AI gateway in public release, catches none of them: recall is 0.000 on every cell of every confusion matrix, on a 1600-sample template baseline through OpenClaw's actual production command-line interface (CLI) and on a ten-LLM cross-model generalisation run. Detecting F1--F4 requires seven specific runtime structures absent from OpenClaw's source tree: a biconditional checker, a hash-chained audit log, an extension admission gate, a two-layer egress guard, a Bell-LaPadula classification policy, a module-signing trust root, and a bootstrap seal. enclawed-oss -- an MIT-licensed drop-in fork that ships all seven -- reaches $P = R = F_1 =$ accuracy $= 1.000$ on the same input. The gap is structural, not parametric: a six-line append-only widening of enclawed-oss's data-loss-prevention (DLP) regex catalog raises per-channel F3 detection by 14.6\% net at unchanged precision; the same edit on OpenClaw has nowhere to land. The harness deliberately exercises real Discord and Telegram channels -- plugin categories the first enclawed release deleted as unsafe -- to show F1--F4 detection extends to those previously-unsafe extensions. With architectural superiority for security and feature parity for extensions, we argue that unhardened agentic-AI runtimes are architecturally obsolete: a strictly better alternative exists, is adoptable today, and the gap requires re-architecture rather than configuration. We invite reviewers to apply the harness to any candidate runtime.

</details>


### [203] [VulKey: Automated Vulnerability Repair Guided by Domain-Specific Repair Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01769)
*Jia Li,Zhuangbin Chen,Yuxin Su,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VulKey是一个基于LLM的自动漏洞修复框架，通过三层层次化抽象（CWE类型、语法动作、语义关键元素）将结构化安全专家知识融入补丁生成过程。在真实世界C/C++数据集PrimeVul上达到31.5%的修复准确率，超过最优基线7.6%，并在Java基准Vul4J上展现了跨语言和跨模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based自动漏洞修复工具未能有效利用结构化的安全知识（如CWE、NVD），要么仅将CWE-ID简单拼接进输入提示词（效果甚微），要么依赖少数样例学习但使用固定且不可泛化的示例，限制了其在真实场景中的有效性。

Method: 提出VulKey框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 专家知识匹配阶段，预测漏洞对应的修复模式；(2) 修复代码生成阶段，使用模式引导的微调LLM生成安全补丁。核心创新在于三层层次化抽象：CWE类型层、语法动作层和语义关键元素层，以比具体示例更泛化、比传统语法模板更富语义的方式捕捉安全修复的本质。

Result: 在PrimeVul数据集上达到31.5%的修复准确率，超过最优基线7.6%，优于VulMaster和GPT-5等工具。在Java基准Vul4J上达到SOTA性能，并展现了跨语言和跨模型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过层次化模式显式建模和整合专家安全知识，是构建更有效和可靠的自动漏洞修复工具的关键步骤。

Abstract: The increasing prevalence of software vulnerabilities highlights the need for effective Automatic Vulnerability Repair (AVR) tools. While LLM-based approaches are promising, they struggle to incorporate structured security knowledge from sources like CWE and NVD. Current methods either use this information superficially by concatenating the CWE-ID into the input prompt, yielding negligible benefits, or rely on few-shot learning with rigid, non-generalizable examples, which limits their effectiveness in real-world scenarios.
  To address this gap, we propose VulKey, an LLM-based AVR framework that leverages a hierarchical abstraction of expert knowledge to guide patch generation. Our novel three-level abstraction formulates repair strategies in terms of CWE type, syntactic actions, and semantic key elements. This approach captures the essence of a security fix with greater generality than concrete examples and more semantic richness than traditional syntax-based templates, overcoming the coverage limitations of prior methods.
  VulKey is implemented as a two-stage pipeline: first, expert knowledge matching predicts an appropriate repair pattern for the vulnerability; second, repair code generation uses a pattern-guided, fine-tuned LLM to produce secure patches.
  On the real-world C/C++ dataset PrimeVul, VulKey achieves 31.5% repair accuracy, surpassing the best baseline by 7.6% and outperforming leading tools such as VulMaster and GPT-5. Moreover, VulKey demonstrates cross-language and cross-model generalizability, with state-of-the-art performance on the Java benchmark Vul4J. These results underscore the importance of structured expert knowledge in advancing AVR effectiveness.
  Our work demonstrates that explicitly modeling and integrating expert security knowledge through hierarchical patterns is a crucial step toward building more effective and reliable AVR tools.

</details>


### [204] [Needle-in-RAG: Prompt-Conditioned Character-Level Traceback of Poisoned Spans in Retrieved Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01782)
*Huining Cui,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAGCharacter，一个针对RAG系统中黑盒字符级投毒溯源的两阶段取证框架。Pass-0记录标准RAG的执行轨迹，Pass-1通过预算约束的反事实掩码和重放实现事件条件下的字符级溯源。实验表明该方法在定位精度与低过度归因之间取得了最佳权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG防御和溯源方法多为段落级别，粒度太粗，无法应对现代攻击中嵌入在良性文本块内的短小伪造声明、触发短语或隐藏指令等有效载荷。

Method: 提出两阶段取证框架RAGCharacter：Pass-0运行标准RAG并记录以提示锚定的执行轨迹；Pass-1在触发迹线上重新进入，通过预算约束的反事实掩码和重放进行事件条件溯源，生成用于取证报告的归因跨度（attribution span）和基于日志轨迹的因果跨度（causal span）。

Result: 在两个QA语料库、五种投毒攻击家族、六种目标LLM以及多个段落级和字符级基线中，RAGCharacter在定位精度和低过度归因之间取得了最佳整体权衡。

Conclusion: 提示条件化的黑盒字符级溯源是可行的，推动RAG取证从文档级怀疑转向更细粒度的证据审计与潜在修复。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves factual grounding by conditioning large language models on retrieved evidence, but it also opens a data-layer attack surface: poisoned corpus entries can steer outputs without changing model parameters. Existing defenses and traceback methods are largely passage-level, which is too coarse for modern attacks whose effective payload may be a short fabricated claim, trigger phrase, or hidden instruction embedded inside an otherwise benign chunk. We study black-box character-level poison traceback in RAG and present RAGCharacter, a two-pass forensic framework that localizes the responsible retrieved span for a concrete misgeneration event. Pass-0 runs standard RAG while logging a prompt-anchored execution trace. Pass-1 re-enters a triggered trace and performs event-conditioned traceback over prompt-used evidence via budgeted counterfactual masking and replay, yielding an attribution span for forensic reporting and a causal span under the logged trace. We further introduce an evaluation protocol that measures both event-level chunk traceback and character-level localization fidelity. Across two QA corpora, five poisoning attack families, six target LLMs, and multiple passage- and character-level baselines, RAGCharacter achieves the best overall trade-off within our benchmark between localization accuracy and low over-attribution. These results suggest that prompt-conditioned, black-box character-level traceback can be feasible, moving RAG forensics from document-level suspicion toward finer-grained evidence auditing and potential remediation.

</details>


### [205] [Repurposing and Evaluating the (In)Feasibility of Dataset Poisoning enabled Watermarking for Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01834)
*Zhiyang Dai,Yansong Gao,Boyu Kuang,Haodong Li,Qi Chang,Gaurav Varshney,Derek Abbott,Anmin Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了对比学习（CL）中数据投毒后门攻击的局限性，发现触发样本与干净样本存在可区分的统计差异。受此启发，将弱后门效应重新利用为数据集知识产权水印，提出基于统一密度度量的统计验证方法及多级水印方案，在保真度、可验证性和鲁棒性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习依赖第三方或互联网数据，面临数据投毒后门攻击的安全威胁，但现有攻击方法的泛化性和鲁棒性研究不足。同时，需要探索如何将后门攻击的负面效应转化为保护数据集IP的正面信号。

Method: 系统评估现有数据投毒后门攻击在CL中的局限性；利用触发样本与干净样本的统计差异，提出基于统一密度度量的统计验证方法；设计适应特征级、软标签或硬标签输出的多级水印方案。

Result: 现有后门攻击存在数据集适应性差、成功率低、可移植性有限、假设条件严苛等问题。部分后门攻击可被重新利用为有效水印，在保真度、可验证性和鲁棒性三者间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 在具有挑战性的对比学习场景中，弱后门效应可以被转化为可靠的信号，用于数据集知识产权保护。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) reduces annotation cost via auto-derived supervisory signals. Since large-scale in-house CL datasets are infeasible, reliance on third-party or internet data is common. Recent studies show CL models are vulnerable to data-poisoning backdoor attacks, but their generalization and robustness are underexplored. We systematically evaluate existing data-poisoning backdoor attacks on CL, revealing limitations: poor dataset adaptability, low success rates, limited portability, and restrictive assumptions (e.g., downstream task knowledge). Interestingly, trigger samples exhibit distinguishable statistical divergence from clean samples, which inspires repurposing it as a watermark for dataset IP protection. Direct repurposing is challenging due to low success rates; we overcome this by statistical verification using a unified density metric. We further propose a multi-level watermarking scheme adapting to feature-level, soft-label, or hard-label outputs in CL. Experiments show some backdoor attacks can be repurposed as effective watermarks with trade-offs among fidelity, verifiability, and robustness. This work demonstrates weak backdoor effects become reliable signals for dataset IP protection in challenging CL settings.

</details>


### [206] [GPU Fingerprinting for Location Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01930)
*Wayne Tee,Jonathan Happel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用硬件指纹替代密钥来识别GPU芯片，以解决物理访问下密钥泄露导致位置验证协议被攻破的安全问题，并在小规模测试中实现了高达100%的再识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于密钥的GPU芯片位置验证协议存在安全漏洞，攻击者通过物理访问提取芯片上的密钥后可以伪造位置信息。为防范未经授权的高级AI模型开发，需要更鲁棒的芯片治理方法。

Method: 提出使用硬件指纹而非密钥来识别GPU，并开发了一种概念验证的GPU指纹识别方法。

Result: 在小规模测试中实现了高达100%的再识别准确率。

Conclusion: 硬件指纹方法可有效替代基于密钥的方案，提升GPU芯片位置验证的安全性，从而更鲁棒地治理GPU芯片以降低未经授权AI模型开发的风险。

Abstract: Robust governance of GPU chips is important for mitigating risks from unauthorized development of advanced AI models. Current methods for monitoring chip location rely on ping-based protocols backed by cryptographic keys stored on-chip. However, these keys can potentially be extracted by adversaries with physical access, compromising the location verification protocol. We address this vulnerability by proposing the use of hardware fingerprints rather than keys to identify GPUs during location verification. In addition, we develop a proof-of-concept GPU fingerprinting methodology that achieves up to 100% re-identification accuracy in small-scale tests.

</details>


### [207] [LAPRAS : Learning-Augmented PRivate Answering for linear query Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01960)
*Pranay Mundra,Adam Sealfon,Ziteng Sun,Quanquan C. Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LAPRAS，一种利用预测信息来改进在线差分隐私查询回答的算法，通过预测集引导隐私预算分配，实现接近离线最优的效果，并在预测不准时优雅降级到基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据库工作负载高度可预测，查询流由重复出现的任务和模板主导，这激发了一种学习增强的在线差分隐私分析方法：利用查询预测来提升隐私预算利用率，同时保持对预测错误的鲁棒性。

Method: LAPRAS使用预测集引导隐私支出：对预测的查询采用离线最优的Matrix Mechanism，剩余查询从残差预算中在线回答。引入Smooth Allocation方法，通过前T=Θ(log²S)个未预测查询形成无偏停时估计量连续校准每个查询的支出。

Result: 在真实数据集上验证了LAPRAS在预测重叠度高时实现接近离线最优的效用，在重叠度低时优雅降级到基线性能水平。

Conclusion: LAPRAS成功实现了预测驱动的隐私预算分配，在预测准确性和鲁棒性之间取得了良好的权衡。

Abstract: Modern database workloads are highly predictable: query streams are dominated by recurring jobs and templates, even when their arrival order is not known in advance. This motivates a learning-augmented view of online differentially private (DP) analytics: can algorithms utilize predictions about which queries will occur to improve utility under a single global privacy budget, while remaining robust when predictions are wrong? We study online DP query answering, where a curator must answer a stream $Q$ of $S$ linear queries arriving in uniformly random order under privacy budget $(ε,δ)$. We present LAPRAS, which assumes access to an oracle that outputs a prediction set of queries likely to appear in the stream and uses it to guide privacy spending. LAPRAS answers predicted queries using the offline-optimal Matrix Mechanism and answers the remaining queries online from a residual budget. To pace spending across an unknown number of unpredicted queries, we introduce Smooth Allocation, which forms an unbiased stopping-time estimate $\widehat{B}$ from the first $T=Θ(\log^2 S)$ unpredicted queries and continuously recalibrates per-query expenditure. Empirically, over two real datasets, we validate the intended consistency--robustness trade-off: LAPRAS achieves near-offline utility under high overlap and degrades gracefully to baseline-level performance when overlap is low.

</details>


### [208] [Trojan Hippo: Weaponizing Agent Memory for Data Exfiltration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01970)
*Debeshee Das,Julien Piet,Darya Kaviani,Luca Beurer-Kellner,Florian Tramèr,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地提出了Trojan Hippo攻击——一种针对LLM智能体长期记忆系统的持久性攻击，攻击者通过一次不可信的工具调用（如恶意邮件）将休眠载荷植入记忆，仅在用户讨论敏感话题时激活以窃取数据。作者构建了动态评估框架，包含OpenEvolve自适应红队基准测试和能力感知的安全/效用分析，在四种记忆后端上测试发现攻击成功率可达85-100%，而现有防御虽能将成功率降至0-5%但存在显著的效用代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统缺乏对持久性攻击的系统性评估，且已有工作仅停留在个别实例演示，缺乏跨异构记忆架构和防御机制的全面评估。

Method: 提出Trojan Hippo攻击方法（休眠载荷+条件触发+数据窃取），构建双组件动态评估框架：基于OpenEvolve的自适应红队基准测试（持续优化攻击绕过防御）和首个能力感知的安全/效用分析框架（支持不同使用场景下的防御部署推理）。在四种记忆后端（显式工具记忆、智能体记忆、RAG、滑动窗口上下文）上进行实验。

Result: Trojan Hippo在OpenAI和Google前沿模型上达到85-100%的攻击成功率，植入的记忆在100次良性会话后仍能激活。四种基础安全防御将攻击成功率降至0-5%，但效用代价因任务需求差异显著。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的安全防御存在显著的安全-效用权衡，实际部署仍面临挑战；所提出的评估框架旨在为这一开放问题提供系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Memory systems enable otherwise-stateless LLM agents to persist user information across sessions, but also introduce a new attack surface. We characterize the Trojan Hippo attack, a class of persistent memory attacks that operates in a more realistic threat model than prior memory poisoning work: the attacker plants a dormant payload into an agent's long-term memory via a single untrusted tool call (e.g., a crafted email), which activates only when the user later discusses sensitive topics such as finance, health, or identity, and exfiltrates high-value personal data to the attacker.
  While anecdotal demonstrations of such attacks have appeared against deployed systems, no prior work systematically evaluates them across heterogeneous memory architectures and defenses.We introduce a dynamic evaluation framework comprising two components: (1) an OpenEvolve-based adaptive red-teaming benchmark that stress-tests defenses and memory backends against continuously refined attacks, and (2) the first capability-aware security/utility analysis for persistent memory systems, enabling principled reasoning about defense deployment across different usage profiles.
  Instantiated on an email assistant across four memory backends (explicit tool memory, agentic memory, RAG, and sliding-window context), Trojan Hippo achieves up to 85-100 percent ASR against current frontier models from OpenAI and Google, with planted memories successfully activating even after 100 benign sessions. We evaluate four memory-system defenses inspired by basic security principles, finding they substantially reduce attack success rates (to as low as 0-5 percent), though at utility costs that vary widely with task requirements. Because of this substantial security-utility tradeoff, the effective real-world deployment of defenses remains an open challenge, which our evaluation framework is specifically designed to address.

</details>


### [209] [Plausible Deniability in Fully Homomorphic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01985)
*Shahzad Ahmad,Stefan Rass,Zahra Seyedi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PD-FHC框架，利用图像和Fredkin门电路实现全同态计算中的可否认加密，使用户能在被胁迫时隐藏真实计算并展示诱饵计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全同态加密（FHE）虽然能保护计算隐私，但在面对胁迫攻击（coercive adversaries）时无法提供可否认性——用户无法否认自己执行了特定计算。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 定义可否认计算媒介（DCM）和可否认计算方案（DCS）的抽象概念；以RGB图像为载体，将多个计算场景（一个真实、多个诱饵）嵌入封面图像的秘密位置；云端对所有像素执行相同的Fredkin门电路操作；在被胁迫时，用户揭示诱饵计算及其可验证结果。

Result: 形式化多轮胁迫博弈，定义存在性区分优势与意图区分优势；证明图像实例化的计算隐私优势为Θ(1/(n-1)!)，存在性隐藏优势可忽略；Python实现（电路规模5-289门，图像128²至512²）表明性能与TFHE相当，且提供了FHE无法提供的可否认性。

Conclusion: PD-FHC框架成功在保持计算隐私的同时增加了可否认性，图像实例化具有可证明的安全性保障和实用性能，为抵抗胁迫攻击的全同态计算提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Plausible Deniability in Fully Homomorphic Computation} (PD-FHC), a framework enabling users to outsource Boolean computations to an untrusted cloud while maintaining both computational privacy against honest-but-curious providers and plausible deniability against coercive adversaries. We define the notion of a \emph{Deniable Computation Medium} (DCM) and a \emph{Deniable Computation Scheme} (DCS) as medium-independent abstractions, then instantiate them using RGB images with Fredkin-gate circuits. Multiple computation scenarios (one real, several decoys) are embedded at secret positions within cover images; the cloud applies identical operations to every pixel, processing all scenarios uniformly. Under coercion, the user reveals a decoy computation with verifiable results while the real computation remains hidden. We formalize multi-round coercion games with existence and intent distinguishing advantages, proving computational privacy with advantage $Θ(1/(n-1)!)$ and negligible existence-hiding advantage for the image instantiation. Our Python implementation, benchmarked across circuit sizes (5--289 gates) and image dimensions ($128^2$ to $512^2$), demonstrates competitive performance with TFHE for Boolean circuits while providing deniability that FHE fundamentally cannot offer.

</details>


### [210] [Obscura: Privacy-Preserving Protocol for the Algorand Blockchain Using LSAG Ring Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02077)
*Navid Azimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Obscura 是一个针对 Algorand 等高性能区块链设计的去中心化隐私协议，通过链上验证的 LSAG 签名实现交易匿名性，并利用 Box Storage 实现 O(1) 承诺成员检查，避免了全局 Merkle 累加器，通过动态操作码预算扩展机制克服了 AVM 的执行限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私增强协议（如基于 zk-SNARKs 的方案）在高吞吐量区块链（如 Algorand）上难以部署，主要受限于严格的每次调用执行预算和全局 Merkle 累加器带来的状态争用问题，亟需一种适应受限智能合约环境的隐私保护方案。

Method: 采用 Linkable Spontaneous Anonymous Group (LSAG) 签名基于 BN254 椭圆曲线实现交易匿名性；提出利用 Algorand 的 Box Storage 实现 O(1) 承诺成员检查以取代全局 Merkle 累加器；设计基于池化内部应用调用的动态操作码预算扩展机制。

Result: 在 Algorand 上实现了实用且高效的签名者匿名隐私保护，无需可信设置或简洁证明。

Conclusion: Obscura 为透明账本提供了鲁棒的隐私层，弥合了高吞吐量区块链架构与加密隐私及选择性可审计性双重需求之间的差距。

Abstract: While public blockchains provide transparent and auditable transaction histories, they inherently compromise user privacy. Existing privacy-enhancing protocols, such as those deployed on Ethereum, typically rely on succinct zero-knowledge proofs (zk-SNARKs) to obscure the transaction graph. However, implementing comparable cryptographic guarantees on high-throughput blockchains like Algorand is challenging due to strict per-call execution budgets and the state contention introduced by global Merkle accumulators. This paper presents Obscura, a decentralized, non-custodial privacy protocol tailored for constrained smart contract environments. Obscura achieves transaction anonymity using Linkable Spontaneous Anonymous Group (LSAG) signatures over the BN254 elliptic curve, verified entirely on-chain. To overcome limitations of the Algorand Virtual Machine (AVM), we introduce a novel state model that leverages Algorand's Box Storage for $O(1)$ commitment membership checks, eliminating the need for global Merkle accumulators, and a dynamic opcode-budget expansion mechanism via pooled inner application calls. Our implementation demonstrates that signer-ambiguous privacy is practical and efficient on Algorand without relying on trusted setups or succinct proofs. Obscura provides a robust privacy layer for transparent ledgers, bridging the gap between high-throughput blockchain architectures and the dual requirements of cryptographic privacy and selective auditability.

</details>


### [211] [Stochastic Modeling of Human-Machine Authentication Channels under Partial Information Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02102)
*Nilesh Chakraborty,Mohammad Zulkernine,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次将PIN输入过程建模为随机的人机通信系统，提出一种上下文条件概率推理框架来量化部分数字泄露情境下的可靠性损失和服务质量退化，使用百万级真实四位数PIN样本在单/双/三数字泄露场景下取得显著效果（单数字预测准确率55.31%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PIN认证安全评估采用二元安全视图（完全安全或完全泄露），忽略了现实IoT场景中部分信息泄露导致的渐进式可靠性退化，缺乏量化评估部分符号暴露下可靠性损失的正式框架。

Method: 将PIN输入建模为随机人机通信系统；提出上下文条件概率推理框架，将缺失数字视为潜在变量，使用平滑条件概率分布结合回退先验进行估计；不显式参数化隐状态转移或发射，而是通过上下文驱动的概率推理近似数字位置间的潜在依赖关系。

Result: 基于百万真实四位数PIN样本，单数字缺失预测准确率55.31%，三数字缺失准确率12.12%；在精确率、召回率和F1分数上均优于标准序列模型基线和经典机器学习模型。

Conclusion: 将PIN输入形式化为一个噪声人机通信信道，实验表明在现实部分暴露条件下存在显著的可靠性退化，为IoT环境下的认证安全性评估提供了新的量化视角。

Abstract: Reliable and secure human-machine communication is fundamental to IoT and cyber-physical ecosystems, where smartphones and wearables commonly serve as authentication controllers. PIN-based authentication can be viewed as a low-bandwidth communication channel through which users transmit numeric credentials under practical constraints. However, conventional evaluations adopt a binary view of security-treating such channels as either fully secure or fully compromised-thereby overlooking the progressive reliability degradation caused by partial information leakage in real-world IoT settings. In this paper, we model the PIN entry process as a stochastic human-IoT communication system and propose a context-conditioned probabilistic inference framework to quantify reliability loss and Quality-of-Service degradation under partial symbol exposure. The proposed approach treats missing digits as latent variables and estimates them using smoothed conditional probability distributions with fallback priors. Unlike traditional sequential models that assume contiguous positional dependencies, the method does not explicitly parameterize hidden-state transitions or emissions; instead, it performs context-driven probabilistic inference to approximate latent dependencies across digit positions. Using over one million real-world four-digit PIN samples, we evaluate single-, double-, and triple-digit leakage scenarios and derive position-dependent reliability metrics. The proposed model achieves up to 55.31% prediction accuracy for one missing digit and 12.12% for three missing digits, while consistently outperforming a standard sequence-model baseline and classical machine learning models in terms of precision, recall, and F1-score. These results formalize PIN entry as a noisy human--IoT communication channel and demonstrate substantial reliability degradation under realistic partial exposure conditions.

</details>


### [212] [SCRIBE: Practical Static Binary Patching via Binary-Aware Recompilation of Decompiled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02121)
*Han Dai,Soumyakant Priyadarshan,Abdullah Imran,Ruoyu Wang,Antonio Bianchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SCRIBE是一个"二进制感知"的补丁框架，通过修复反编译器输出中的语法和语义不准确问题，实现了无需原始源代码即可对二进制文件进行源码级补丁操作。实验表明，SCRIBE修复了Hex-Rays反编译器约81%的错误函数，成功修补了14个真实CVE中的13个，用户研究显示使用SCRIBE的补丁成功率从3.7%提升至100%，且三个大语言模型在框架辅助下均达到100%的自动化补丁成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法获得源代码或原始工具链时，对二进制文件进行补丁非常困难，因为需要直接编辑底层汇编代码。虽然可以通过反编译、在源码级别应用补丁、然后重新编译的方式替代，但现代反编译器的输出存在大量语法和语义不准确问题，阻碍了这一工作流程的有效性。

Method: SCRIBE提出"二进制感知"重编译方法，通过直接从原始二进制中提取信息来修复反编译器输出中的语义不准确问题，从而同时提高重编译成功率和正确性。

Result: SCRIBE解决了Hex-Rays反编译器约81%的错误函数；成功修补了14个真实CVE中的13个；用户研究中SCRIBE组补丁成功率为100%（对照组仅3.7%）；三个大语言模型在SCRIBE框架辅助下均实现100%自动化补丁成功率。

Conclusion: SCRIBE使得无需原始源代码即可对二进制文件进行可访问且可靠的源码级补丁操作，为二进制安全补丁领域提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: When source code or the original toolchain is unavailable, patching binaries is difficult because it requires editing low-level assembly code directly. As an alternative, one can decompile the binary, apply the patch at the source level, and then recompile the modified code. However, as this paper demonstrates, this workflow is hindered by pervasive syntactic and semantic inaccuracies in the output of modern decompilers, many of which prior work has overlooked. To address these challenges, we present SCRIBE, a patching framework that handles syntactic and semantic issues in decompiled code, improving both recompilation success and correctness. SCRIBE's novel "binary-aware" recompilation approach repairs semantic inaccuracies in decompiler output by leveraging information extracted directly from the original binary. In our evaluation, SCRIBE resolved approximately 81% of previously incorrect functions produced by the Hex-Rays decompiler, demonstrating the effectiveness of its approach. Moreover, we show that, using SCRIBE, it is possible to patch 13 of 14 real-world CVEs without access to the original source code and without performing any manual binary editing. To further validate our findings, we conducted a user study with 18 participants. Using SCRIBE, participants achieved 100% patching success, compared to 3.7% without it. Finally, we asked three large language models to generate source-level patches via SCRIBE; all three achieved 100% success when using the framework, demonstrating its potential to enable fully automated patching. Overall, these results indicate that SCRIBE makes source-level patching of binaries accessible and reliable, even without access to the original source.

</details>


### [213] [On the Privacy of LLMs: An Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02255)
*Karima Makhlouf,Lamiaa Basyoni,Syed Khaderi,Gabriel Marquez,Peter Sotomango,Mahmoud Awawdah,Sami Zhioua*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了统一威胁模型和符号体系，复现了代表性隐私攻击（MIA、AIA、DEA、BA），并通过结构化消融实验评估了模型架构、规模、数据集特征和检索配置等关键因素对攻击效果的影响。成员推理攻击（尤其是掩码变体）和后门攻击表现强劲，而属性推理和数据提取攻击准确率较低但风险依然显著，表明隐私风险高度依赖于系统设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM的隐私攻击研究（成员推理、属性推理、数据提取、后门攻击）通常被孤立分析，缺乏在统一框架下理解它们受常见系统因素影响的系统性研究。

Method: 提出统一威胁模型和符号体系，复现代表性隐私攻击，并进行结构化消融实验，系统评估模型架构、规模、数据集特征和检索配置等关键因素的影响。

Result: 成员推理攻击（尤其是掩码变体）表现出强且可靠的信号；后门攻击因触发式机制实现持续高成功率；属性推理和数据提取攻击准确率较低但仍对敏感个人信息构成显著风险。

Conclusion: LLM系统中的隐私风险高度依赖于上下文和设计选择，需要整体性评估和知情部署实践。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in interactive and retrieval-augmented settings, raising significant privacy concerns. While attacks such as Membership Inference (MIA), Attribute Inference (AIA), Data Extraction (DEA), and Backdoor Attacks (BA) have been studied, they are typically analyzed in isolation, leaving a gap in understanding their behavior under common system factors. In this paper, we introduce a unified threat model and notation, reproduce a representative set of privacy attacks, and conduct a structured ablation study to evaluate the impact of key factors such as model architecture, scale, dataset characteristics, and retrieval configuration. Our analysis reveals clear differences across attack types. Membership inference attacks, particularly mask-based variants, exhibit strong and reliable signals, while backdoor attacks achieve consistently high success rates due to their trigger-based nature. In contrast, attribute inference and data extraction attacks remain more challenging, resulting in lower accuracy, yet they pose significant risks as they target sensitive personal information. Overall, these results highlight that privacy risks in LLM systems are highly context-dependent and driven by design choices, emphasizing the need for holistic evaluation and informed deployment practices.

</details>


### [214] [Optimal Privacy-Utility Trade-Offs in LDP: Functional and Geometric Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02319)
*Seung-Hyun Nam,Hyun-Young Park,Si-Hyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的理論框架，系统性地刻画了局部差分隐私（LDP）下通用统计决策问题的最优隐私-效用权衡（PUT）及最优LDP信道。通过识别风险函数的关键性质（数据处理不等式、直和拟凸性、凹性、对称不变性），将优化域约简至有限维多面体，并利用几何对应关系实现PUT的计算可解性。在具有传递群对称性的问题中，可进一步得到PUT的闭式解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDP框架下最优隐私-效用权衡的表征目前高度碎片化，依赖逐问题、逐案例分析，缺乏统一理论。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立系统性理论框架。

Method: 1) 识别贝叶斯风险与极小化风险作为LDP信道函数的关键性质（DPI、直和拟凸性/可加性、凹性、对称不变性）；2) 利用这些性质约简优化域；3) 基于凸几何建立Blackwell序下最大LDP信道与有限维多面体的一一对应，通过顶点枚举或线性规划实现PUT计算；4) 在传递群对称性条件下推导PUT解析表达式。

Result: 1) 建立了最优PUT的计算可解性框架；2) 在对称性条件下得到闭式解；3) 框架适用于风险最小化及信息论度量（互信息、f-散度、Fisher信息）最大化；4) 恢复/加强了若干已知结果，并推导了此前未解决任务的PUT解析表达式。

Conclusion: 本文提供了LDP下最优隐私-效用权衡的统一理论表征，兼具理论完备性与计算可行性，为隐私保护统计决策问题提供了系统化的分析工具。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) has emerged as a gold-standard framework for privacy-preserving data analysis. However, characterizing the optimal privacy-utility trade-off (PUT) and the corresponding optimal LDP channels remains largely fragmented, relying on problem-specific, case-by-case analyses. In this work, we develop a unified theoretical framework that systematically characterizes the optimal PUT and optimal LDP channels for general privacy-preserving statistical decision-making problems. We first identify key functional properties of Bayesian and minimax risks as functions of the LDP channel, including the data processing inequality (DPI), direct-sum quasi-convexity (or additivity), concavity, and symmetry invariance. Leveraging these properties, we reduce the optimization domain required to compute the optimal PUT. Additionally, building on convex geometric insights, we establish a one-to-one correspondence between maximal LDP channels under the Blackwell order and a finite-dimensional polytope, yielding an exact geometric characterization. This result renders the optimal PUT computationally tractable via vertex enumeration or linear programming. Furthermore, when the underlying problem exhibits symmetries characterized by a transitive group action, we derive an exact analytic expression for the optimal PUT, leading to closed-form solutions without numerical optimization. Our framework applies broadly beyond risk minimization, encompassing the maximization of information-theoretic measures such as mutual information, $f$-divergences, and Fisher information over LDP channels. We demonstrate the efficacy of our theoretical framework by recovering or strengthening several known results, and deriving exact analytic expressions for the optimal PUTs in specific tasks that were previously unaddressed.

</details>


### [215] [Privacy Preserving Machine Learning Workflow: from Anonymization to Personalized Differential Privacy Budgets in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02372)
*Judith Sáinz-Pardo Díaz,Álvaro López García*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对敏感表格数据的隐私保护联邦学习工作流，结合了匿名化和差分隐私技术，引入了客户端漂移的形式化定义及检测方法以缓解投毒攻击，并基于重识别风险指标设计了客户端个性化隐私预算分配方法。实验表明，个性化预算方案在模型性能上优于固定全局预算方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能避免数据集中化，但仍面临数据完整性和隐私挑战，现有方法缺乏对客户端异质性风险的细粒度处理。

Method: 1) 结合匿名化与差分隐私的隐私保护工作流；2) 客户端漂移的形式化定义与检测机制；3) 基于重识别风险度量的个性化隐私预算分配方法。

Result: 在公开医疗数据集上，个性化隐私预算方案在两项误差指标上均优于固定全局差分隐私预算方案。

Conclusion: 个性化隐私预算分配在保障隐私的同时能提升模型性能，为敏感表格数据的联邦学习提供了一种实用的隐私-效用权衡方案。

Abstract: The growing development of artificial intelligence based solutions, together with privacy legislation, has driven the rise of the so-called privacy preserving machine learning architectures, such as federated learning. While federated learning enables model training on decentralized data preventing their sharing and centralization, it still faces several challenges related to data integrity and privacy. This paper presents a comprehensive privacy preserving federated learning workflow for sensitive tabular data, including anonymization and differential privacy techniques. We also introduce a formal definition for the concept of client drift, together with ways of detecting it to mitigate poisoning attacks. Then, we detail a complete methodology for assigning personalized privacy budgets for global differential privacy to the different clients participating in the network, based on a re-identification risk metric. The proposed methodology is presented and tested on an openly available dataset of medical records. Within the experimental setup we show that the approach based on personalized budgets, compared to the architecture including global differential privacy with fixed privacy budget, achieves a better model performance in terms of two error metrics.

</details>


### [216] [Fight Poison with Poison: Enhancing Robustness in Few-shot Machine-Generated Text Detection with Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02374)
*Wenjing Duan,Qi Zhou,Yuanfan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出REACT框架，通过耦合基于RAG的人性化攻击器与对比学习检测器进行对抗训练，在少样本场景下提升机器生成文本检测的准确性和鲁棒性，实验表明F1提升4.95点，攻击成功率降低3.66个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器生成文本检测器在少样本场景下表现不佳，且易受到对抗性、人性化攻击的威胁，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 采用威胁建模视角，提出REACT对抗训练框架：攻击端利用检索增强生成（RAG）构造高度类人的对抗样本；检测端通过对比学习目标函数从对抗样本中学习，稳定少样本表示学习并增强鲁棒性；攻击器与检测器交替更新实现协同进化。

Result: 在4个数据集、4种样本量、3个随机种子下，REACT相比8个SOTA检测器平均F1提升4.95点，在4种强攻击下平均攻击成功率降低3.66个百分点。

Conclusion: 从攻击者视角研究检测器漏洞并进行针对性对抗训练，能有效提升少样本MGT检测的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Machine-generated text (MGT) detection is critical for regulating online information ecosystems, yet existing detectors often underperform in few-shot settings and remain vulnerable to adversarial, humanizing attacks. To build accurate and robust detectors under limited supervision, we adopt a threat-modeling perspective and study detector vulnerabilities from an attacker's viewpoint under an output-only black-box setting. Motivated by this perspective, we propose RAG-GuidEd Attacker Strengthens ConTrastive Few-shot Detector (REACT), an adversarial training framework that improves both few-shot detection performance and robustness against attacks. REACT couples a humanization-oriented attacker with a target detector: the attacker leverages retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to craft highly human-like adversarial examples to evade detection, while the detector learns from these adversaries with a contrastive objective to stabilize few-shot representation learning and enhance robustness. We alternately update the attacker and the detector to enable their co-evolution. Experiments on 4 datasets with 4 shot sizes and 3 random seeds show that REACT improves average detection F1 by 4.95 points over 8 state-of-the-art (SOTA) detectors and reduces the average attack success rate (ASR) under 4 strong attacks by 3.66 percentage points.

</details>


### [217] [Design and Performance Evaluation of a BLE-Based IoT Authentication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02381)
*Nitesh Yadav,Vashisht Kumar,Sachin Kadam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于nRF5340 BLE节点，本文设计了一个集成PIN认证、温度传感和RSSI性能评估的物联网安全通信系统，实现低延迟、高安全的无线数据交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLE在物联网中广泛应用但安全和通信可靠性仍面临挑战，现有方案在轻量级设备上缺乏兼顾安全认证、实时传感和性能评估的集成方案。

Method: 采用工业级nRF5340开发套件，构建外设节点（含键盘输入PIN）和中央节点（含LCD显示）的BLE通信架构；外设节点集成温度传感器，通过PIN认证后传输传感数据；收集不同场景下的RSSI值评估信号强度随距离变化规律。

Result: RSSI值在近距离时信号强，随距离增加呈对数衰减模式；系统具有低延迟特性，支持实时输入输出；PIN认证机制有效保障安全，防止未授权使用。

Conclusion: 该系统成功集成了通信、传感与安全功能，适用于智能门禁控制和无线监控系统，包括家庭自动化场景。

Abstract: Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) is widely used in modern IoT systems because it consumes very little power, saves energy, and allows for simple device connectivity; however, maintaining security and communication reliability remains a challenge. In this paper, an authentication system is designed using industry-grade BLE-enabled nodes (nRF5340 development kit) that include a peripheral node with a keypad for entering a PIN and a central node with an LCD display. The entered PIN is sent wirelessly from the peripheral node to the central node via BLE technology, where it is verified in real time and displayed as correct or incorrect. Next, only after successful authentication can the peripheral node send data to the central node. In addition to authentication, the peripheral node can measure temperature in real time using the temperature sensor interfaced to it and send it wirelessly to the central node, where it can be displayed on the LCD interface. Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) values are collected during experiments under various scenarios to evaluate the system's performance. We see that the signal strength (measured in terms of RSSI values) is strong at close range but weak as distance increases, indicating a decaying logarithmic pattern. The system also has low latency, which allows for quick input and output, and it uses PIN-based authentication to ensure security and prevent misuse. The entire system seamlessly integrates communication, sensing, and security, making it suitable for smart access control and wireless monitoring systems, including home automation.

</details>


### [218] [Differentially Private Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02391)
*Bernd Finkbeiner,Frederik Scheerer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在流式监控规范中自动实现差分隐私的方法，通过分析时序依赖关系并在规范中策略性地注入校准噪声，同时利用树状机制减轻聚合算子的精度损失，在保护隐私的同时保持监控输出的实用性。案例研究验证了该方法在公共交通监控中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代流式监控系统会收集系统运行时的详细统计信息，在隐私敏感环境下存在敏感信息泄露风险。差分隐私是保护敏感信息的主流方法，但将其集成到运行时监控中面临挑战：时序算子会导致单个输入值随时间影响多个输出，造成隐私信息的重复泄露。

Method: 通过分析时序依赖关系自动在监控规范中施加差分隐私；识别噪声注入的策略性位置；利用树状机制降低聚合算子噪声注入带来的精度损失。

Result: 在公共交通监控案例研究中验证了方法的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能自动在流式监控规范中实现差分隐私保护，通过策略性噪声注入和树状机制在隐私保护与输出效用之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Modern stream-based monitors collect detailed statistics of the runtime behavior of the system under observation. If the system runs in a privacy-sensitive context, this poses the risk of disclosing sensitive information. Differential privacy is the state-of-the-art approach for protecting sensitive information, however, integrating it into runtime monitoring is challenging: temporal operators can cause individual input values to influence multiple outputs over time, leading to repeated disclosure of private information. We propose an approach that automatically enforces differential privacy in stream-based monitoring specifications by analyzing temporal dependencies and injecting carefully calibrated noise into the specification. To preserve the utility of the outputs, we identify strategically chosen positions in the specification for noise injection and leverage tree-based mechanisms to mitigate the accuracy loss caused by noise injected into aggregation operators. We demonstrate the practicality and effectiveness of our approach in a case study on monitoring public transportation usage.

</details>


### [219] [VertMark: A Unified Training-Free Robust Watermarking Framework for Vertical Domain Pre-trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02557)
*Cong Kong,Xin Cheng,Zhaoxia Yin,Shuai Li,Jie Zhang,Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VertMark，首个无需训练、鲁棒且统一的多垂直领域预训练语言模型（VPLM）版权水印框架。通过基于低频触发词与高频领域相关词之间隐语义等价的免训练参数替换策略嵌入所有权编码水印，无需额外训练。实验在医疗、金融、法律领域的文本理解与生成任务上验证了高效嵌入和可靠验证，且对模型性能影响极小，并展现了强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VPLM版权验证方法主要依赖后门水印嵌入，但存在需额外训练、水印嵌入效率低、缺乏面向多垂直领域的可扩展设计等局限性。

Method: 提出VertMark框架，利用低频触发词与高频领域相关词之间的隐语义等价性，通过无需训练的（training-free）参数替换策略嵌入所有权编码水印。

Result: 在医疗、金融、法律领域的文本理解与生成下游任务上实现高效水印嵌入和可靠验证，对模型性能影响极小；对剪枝、量化等攻击具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VertMark为多垂直领域VPLM版权保护提供了首个统一、无需训练且鲁棒的水印框架，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: With the application of vertical domain pre-trained language models (VPLMs) in specialized fields such as medical, finance, and law, model parameters and inference capabilities have become important digital assets.
